# Tianxia's expansion to the seclusion mechanic
# by Silverswee(e)per
# Some of the vanilla events have been reworked and remain in use; see rip_seclusion_events.txt for those

namespace = tianxia

# IDs 1801-2200 reserved



##### Seclusion event setup

### Setup - food gathering

# on_death if you have NOT gathered food, make sure your heir gets on that
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1801
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death
	
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	only_rulers = yes
	
	trigger = {
		in_seclusion = yes
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = seclusion_food_gathering_decision_made
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		player_heir = {
			change_variable = {
				which = seclusion_food
				which = ROOT
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_playable = yes
						ROOT = {
							is_playable = yes
						}
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.1802 days = 3 } # Fire the event for your heir
			}
		}
	}
}

# Do you wish to have someone gather extra food?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1802
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1802
	picture = GFX_evt_rider_adventure
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	only_playable = yes
	
	trigger = {
		in_seclusion = yes
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = seclusion_food_gathering_decision_made
		}
		any_courtier = {
			is_adult = yes
			is_landed = no
			prisoner = no
			is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
			is_maimed_trigger = no
		}
		any_demesne_province = {
			NOT = {
				has_province_modifier = recent_food_gathering
			}
		}
	}
	
	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				in_seclusion = yes
				NOT = {
					has_character_flag = seclusion_food_gathering_decision_made
				}
			}
			
			set_character_flag = seclusion_food_gathering_decision_made
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = seclusion_food_gathering_decision_made
		
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = recent_food_gathering
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				has_disease = no
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = seclusion_food_province
		}
		
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_landed = no
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				is_maimed_trigger = no
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				# Prefer someone interesting
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
				}
				# With interesting traits
				OR = {
					trait = gardener
					trait = hunter
					trait = gregarious
					trait = temperate
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = kind
					trait = cruel
					trait = proud
					trait = humble
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = just
					trait = craven
					AND = {
						trait = zealous
						NOT = {
							religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				# Prefer someone interesting or someone with interesting traits
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
					trait = gardener
					trait = hunter
					trait = gregarious
					trait = temperate
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = kind
					trait = cruel
					trait = proud
					trait = humble
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = just
					trait = craven
					AND = {
						trait = zealous
						NOT = {
							religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = gather_food_option_1
		}
		
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_landed = no
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				is_maimed_trigger = no
				NOT = {
					character = event_target:gather_food_option_1
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				# Prefer someone interesting
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
				}
				# With interesting traits
				OR = {
					trait = gardener
					trait = hunter
					trait = gregarious
					trait = temperate
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = kind
					trait = cruel
					trait = proud
					trait = humble
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = just
					trait = craven
					AND = {
						trait = zealous
						NOT = {
							religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				# Prefer someone interesting or someone with interesting traits
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
					trait = gardener
					trait = hunter
					trait = gregarious
					trait = temperate
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = kind
					trait = cruel
					trait = proud
					trait = humble
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = just
					trait = craven
					AND = {
						trait = zealous
						NOT = {
							religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = gather_food_option_2
		}
		
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_landed = no
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				is_maimed_trigger = no
				NOR = {
					character = event_target:gather_food_option_1
					character = event_target:gather_food_option_2
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				# Prefer someone interesting
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
				}
				# With interesting traits
				OR = {
					trait = gardener
					trait = hunter
					trait = gregarious
					trait = temperate
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = kind
					trait = cruel
					trait = proud
					trait = humble
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = just
					trait = craven
					AND = {
						trait = zealous
						NOT = {
							religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				# Prefer someone interesting or someone with interesting traits
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
					trait = gardener
					trait = hunter
					trait = gregarious
					trait = temperate
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = kind
					trait = cruel
					trait = proud
					trait = humble
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = just
					trait = craven
					AND = {
						trait = zealous
						NOT = {
							religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = gather_food_option_3
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1802 # Let's send the first courtier!
		
		event_target:gather_food_option_1 = {
			show_scope_change = no
			
			save_event_target_as = gathering_food
			
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			
			opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
				months = 1
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1803 } # Decide on approach
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = event_target:gather_food_option_1
					NOT = {
						trait = trusting
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				true_religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
				NOT = {
					religion = event_target:gather_food_option_1
				}
				event_target:gather_food_option_1 = {
					trait = zealous
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:gather_food_option_1
					value >= 50
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = temperate
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:gather_food_option_2 = {
				always = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1802 # Let's send the second courtier!
		
		event_target:gather_food_option_2 = {
			show_scope_change = no
			
			save_event_target_as = gathering_food
			
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			
			opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
				months = 1
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1803 } # Decide on approach
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = event_target:gather_food_option_2
					NOT = {
						trait = trusting
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				true_religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
				NOT = {
					religion = event_target:gather_food_option_2
				}
				event_target:gather_food_option_2 = {
					trait = zealous
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:gather_food_option_2
					value >= 50
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = temperate
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:gather_food_option_3 = {
				always = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1802 # Let's send the third courtier!
		
		event_target:gather_food_option_3 = {
			show_scope_change = no
			
			save_event_target_as = gathering_food
			
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			
			opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
				months = 1
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1803 } # Decide on approach
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = event_target:gather_food_option_3
					NOT = {
						trait = trusting
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				true_religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
				NOT = {
					religion = event_target:gather_food_option_3
				}
				event_target:gather_food_option_3 = {
					trait = zealous
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:gather_food_option_3
					value >= 50
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = temperate
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_1802 # I have enough food...
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						trait = gluttonous
					}
					AND = {
						is_rival = event_target:gather_food_option_1
						OR = {
							event_target:gather_food_option_2 = {
								always = no
							}
							is_rival = event_target:gather_food_option_2
						}
						OR = {
							event_target:gather_food_option_3 = {
								always = no
							}
							is_rival = event_target:gather_food_option_3
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = gluttonous
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = proud
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = hedonist
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = decadent
			}
		}
	}
}

# Food gatherer leaves seclusion and decides on their approach
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1803
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		clear_flags_with_prefix = getting_food_
		
		random_list = {
			33 = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						trait = temperate
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 10
					trait = cruel
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.1
					trait = kind
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 5
					trait = arbitrary
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.2
					trait = just
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.1
					trait = craven
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = proud
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					trait = humble
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 10
					trait = zealous
					NOT = {
						true_religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
					}
				}
				
				set_character_flag = getting_food_harsh
			}
			
			34 = {
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = hunter
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = gardener
				}
				
				set_character_flag = getting_food_average
			}
			
			33 = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						trait = gluttonous
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = hunter
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = gardener
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 10
					trait = kind
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.1
					trait = cruel
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 5
					trait = just
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.2
					trait = arbitrary
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = humble
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					trait = proud
				}
				
				set_character_flag = getting_food_mild
			}
		}
		
		character_event = { id = tianxia.1804 days = 10 random = 10 } # Determine outcome
	}
}

# Determine outcome
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1804
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_landed = yes
					host = {
						NOT = {
							reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			any_opinion_modifier_target = {
				limit = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.1805 } # Inform liege of return of non-specific food gatherer
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			# Check if we got the disease
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						event_target:seclusion_food_province = {
							has_disease = yes
						}
						host = {
							capital_scope = {
								has_disease = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							trait = brave
						}
						
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							event_target:seclusion_food_province = {
								has_disease = yes
							}
							host = {
								capital_scope = {
									has_disease = yes
								}
							}
						}
						
						set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
						
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1809 days = 15 random = 15 } # Develop disease
					}
					
					50 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							trait = craven
						}
						
						# Nothing
					}
				}
			}
			
			# Then determine the outcome
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						has_character_flag = getting_food_harsh
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						trait = zealous
						NOT = {
							true_religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 10
						trait = gregarious
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						trait = kind
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						trait = humble
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						trait = just
					}
					
					any_opinion_modifier_target = {
						limit = {
							reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
							}
						}
						
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1806 } # Inform liege of normal return
					}
				}
				
				25 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.5
						has_character_flag = getting_food_mild
					}
					
					any_opinion_modifier_target = {
						limit = {
							reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
							}
						}
						
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1807 } # Inform liege of upset peasants
					}
				}
				
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						NOT = {
							has_character_flag = getting_food_mild
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						trait = cruel
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						trait = proud
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						trait = arbitrary
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						trait = craven
					}
					
					any_opinion_modifier_target = {
						limit = {
							reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
							}
						}
						
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1808 } # Inform liege of death and upset peasants
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Food gatherer is landed or not in the realm - inform liege that a lesser amount of food was returned
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1805
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1805
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	only_playable = yes
	
	trigger = {
		in_seclusion = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1805 # Very well...
		
		reverse_remove_opinion = {
			who = FROM
			modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
		}
		
		seclusion_food_from_province_smaller_effect = yes
		
		# The disease might still spread...
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						event_target:seclusion_food_province = {
							has_disease = yes
						}
						host = {
							capital_scope = {
								has_disease = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							event_target:seclusion_food_province = {
								has_disease = yes
							}
							host = {
								capital_scope = {
									has_disease = yes
								}
							}
						}
						
						set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
					}
					
					75 = {
						# Nothing
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		update_seclusion_food_modifier_effect = yes
	}
}

# Food gatherer returns - normal
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1806
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1806
	picture = GFX_evt_emissary
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	only_playable = yes
	
	trigger = {
		in_seclusion = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1806 # Excellent!
		
		reverse_remove_opinion = {
			who = FROM
			modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
		}
		
		FROM = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = in_seclusion
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		
		seclusion_food_from_province_effect = yes
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						has_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
					}
				}
				
				set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = paranoid
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = arbitrary
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				trait = paranoid
				trait = cruel
				trait = arbitrary
				is_rival = FROM
			}
			is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1806 # Great, but you're not getting back in!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			
			add_trait_partially_cruel_effect = yes
			add_trait_partially_arbitrary_effect = yes
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
				e_china = {
					holder_scope = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						is_close_relative = FROM
						dynasty = FROM
						NOT = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
			detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
		}
		
		FROM = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_friend = ROOT
				}
				
				remove_friend = ROOT
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_lover = ROOT
				}
				
				remove_lover = ROOT
			}
			
			opinion = {
				name = expelled_me_from_seclusion
				who = ROOT
				years = 2
			}
		}
		
		seclusion_food_from_province_effect = yes
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						has_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
					}
				}
				
				random = {
					chance = 50
					set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_friend = FROM
					is_lover = FROM
					AND = {
						is_close_relative = FROM
						NOT = {
							is_rival = FROM
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = just
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		update_seclusion_food_modifier_effect = yes
	}
}

# Food gatherer returns - peasants more upset than expected
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1807
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1807
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	only_playable = yes
	
	trigger = {
		in_seclusion = yes
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1807 # Not entirely unexpected...
		
		reverse_remove_opinion = {
			who = FROM
			modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
		}
		
		FROM = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = in_seclusion
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		
		event_target:seclusion_food_province = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = peasants_upset
				years = 2
			}
		}
		
		seclusion_food_from_province_effect = yes
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						has_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
					}
				}
				
				set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				trait = paranoid
				trait = cruel
				trait = arbitrary
				is_rival = FROM
			}
			is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1807 # Great, but you're not getting back in!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			
			add_trait_partially_cruel_effect = yes
			add_trait_partially_arbitrary_effect = yes
		}
		
		seclusion_food_from_province_effect = yes
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						has_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
					}
				}
				
				random = {
					chance = 50
					set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
				e_china = {
					holder_scope = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						is_close_relative = FROM
						dynasty = FROM
						NOT = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
			detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
		}
		
		FROM = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_friend = ROOT
				}
				
				remove_friend = ROOT
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_lover = ROOT
				}
				
				remove_lover = ROOT
			}
			
			opinion = {
				name = expelled_me_from_seclusion
				who = ROOT
				years = 2
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_friend = FROM
					is_lover = FROM
					AND = {
						is_close_relative = FROM
						NOT = {
							is_rival = FROM
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = just
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		update_seclusion_food_modifier_effect = yes
	}
}

	}

# Food gatherer develops disease - food gatherer
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1809
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		is_ill = no
		num_of_symptoms < 1
	}
	
	immediate = {
		give_epidemic_disease_effect = yes
	}
}

# on_death of ruler, inherit current food and the food gatherer opinion
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1810
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death
	
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	only_rulers = yes
	
	trigger = {
		in_seclusion = yes
		has_character_flag = seclusion_food_gathering_decision_made
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_opinion_modifier_target = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
					}
				}
			}
			
			any_opinion_modifier_target = {
				limit = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
					}
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = food_gatherer
			}
			
			player_heir = {
				change_variable = {
					which = seclusion_food
					which = ROOT
				}
				
				event_target:food_gatherer = {
					opinion = {
						who = PREV
						modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			player_heir = {
				change_variable = {
					which = seclusion_food
					which = ROOT
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_character_flag = seclusion_tombola_setup
						}
					}
					
					set_character_flag = seclusion_tombola_setup
				
					character_event = { id = tianxia.1821 days = 30 } # Food tombola
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# on_death of food gatherer, fire a worse food gathering event for the liege
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1811
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death
	
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	
	trigger = {
		host = {
			in_seclusion = yes
		}
		has_any_opinion_modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
		NOT = {
			has_character_flag = killed_by_peasants
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_ill = yes
				death_murder_trigger = no
				death_murder_unknown_trigger = no
				death_murder_known_trigger = no
				death_execution_trigger = no
				death_sacrificed_trigger = no
				death_accident_trigger = no
				death_suicide_trigger = no
				martyrs_death_trigger = no
				death_no_trace_trigger = no
			}
		
			any_opinion_modifier_target = {
				limit = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.1812 days = 1 } # Inform liege
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			any_opinion_modifier_target = {
				limit = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.1813 days = 1 } # Inform liege
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

	}
}

			}
			
			10 = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						trait = diligent
					}
					NOT = {
						has_character_flag = seclusion_type_option_relax
					}
					is_adult = yes
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 10
					trait = slothful
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 10
					trait = proud
				}
				
				set_character_flag = seclusion_type_option_relax
			}
			
			100 = {
				# Nothing
			}
		}
		
		set_character_flag = seclusion_type_set
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = seclusion_type_option_prayer
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1814 # We shall pray for things to get better!
		
		set_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_prayer
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = plentiful_food
			}
			remove_character_modifier = plentiful_food
		}
		
		any_courtier = {
			limit = {
				has_character_modifier = plentiful_food
			}
			remove_character_modifier = plentiful_food
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = cynical
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = zealous
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = seclusion_type_option_party
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1814 # We should enjoy ourselves!
		
		set_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_party
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				religion_group = muslim
			}
			
			decadence = 1
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = seclusion_food
					value >= 1
				}
			}
			
			add_character_modifier = {
				name = plentiful_food
				duration = -1
			}

			any_courtier = {
				show_scope_change = no

				limit = { in_seclusion = yes }

				add_character_modifier = {
					name = plentiful_food
					duration = -1
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = gluttonous
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = decadent
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = hedonist
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = drunkard
			}
		}
	}

# on_death, let heir decide on their seclusion type
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1815
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death
	
	has_dlc = "Reapers"
	only_rulers = yes
	
	trigger = {
		in_seclusion = yes
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				player_heir = {
					OR = {
						is_landed = no
						NAND = {
							has_character_flag = seclusion_type_set
							has_character_flag = seclusion_tombola_setup
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			player_heir = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							is_playable = yes
							ROOT = {
								is_playable = yes
							}
						}
						NOR = {
							has_character_flag = seclusion_type_set
							has_character_flag = end_seclusion # ROOT was murdered to end seclusion
						}
					}
						
					character_event = { id = tianxia.1816 days = 1 } # Decide on seclusion type
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							has_character_flag = seclusion_tombola_setup
							has_character_flag = end_seclusion # ROOT was murdered to end seclusion
						}
					}
					
					set_character_flag = seclusion_tombola_setup
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								is_playable = yes
								ROOT = {
									is_playable = yes
								}
							}
						}
						
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1820 days = 60 random = 60 } # New tombola event
					}
					
					else = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1820 days = 100 random = 100 } # New tombola event
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# It has been a while - continue with your current approach or not?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1818
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1818
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1818 # Perhaps it is time...
		
		character_event = { id = tianxia.1819 } # Pick a new approach
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_temperate
					check_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value < 5
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				OR = {
					has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_temperate
					has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_gardening
				}
				check_variable = {
					which = seclusion_food
					value < 10
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1818 # I'll keep going with this approach...
		
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_type_set
		set_character_flag = seclusion_type_set
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = stubborn
			}
		}
	}
}

# New tombola event - copies some stuff from RIP.12099, but also adds a bunch of weights and a bunch of new stuff
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1820
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	only_rulers = yes
	
	trigger = {
		in_seclusion = yes
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			20 = {
				trigger = {
					location = { has_disease = yes }

					is_ill = no
					num_of_symptoms < 1

					OR = {
						has_character_flag = exposed_to_outside

						any_host_courtier = {
							in_seclusion = yes
							has_epidemic_disease_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}

				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 1.5

					any_host_courtier = {
						in_seclusion = yes
						developing_epidemic_disease_trigger = yes
					}
				}

				give_epidemic_disease_effect = yes
			}

			80 = { }
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_prayer
						OR = {
							has_secret_religion = yes
							is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_party
						OR = {
							trait = temperate
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
						}
						NOR = {
							trait = decadent
							trait = hedonist
							trait = drunkard
						}
					}
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_busy
						OR = {
							trait = slothful
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_temperate
						OR = {
							trait = gluttonous
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					AND = {	
						has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_gardening
						OR = {
							trait = slothful
							NOT = {
								trait = gardener
							}
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					AND = {
						has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_sneaky
						OR = {
							trait = honest
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					AND = {	
						has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_relax
						OR = {
							trait = diligent
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			random_list = {
				# Food guard events
				1000 = {
					trigger = {
						is_playable = yes
						is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
						
						OR = {
							NOT = {
								has_character_flag = food_guard
							}
							had_character_flag = { flag = food_guard months = 6 }
						}
						
						OR = {
							check_variable = {
								which = seclusion_food
								value < 5
							}
							
							AND = {
								NOT = {
									trait = trusting
								}
								check_variable = {
									which = seclusion_food
									value < 10
								}
							}
							AND = {
								has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_temperate
								check_variable = {
									which = seclusion_food
									value < 10
								}
							}
							
							AND = {
								has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_temperate
								NOT = {
									trait = trusting
								}
								check_variable = {
									which = seclusion_food
									value < 15
								}
							}
							
							trait = paranoid
						}
						
						NOT = {
							any_courtier = {
								in_seclusion = yes
								prisoner = no
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
								reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
									who = ROOT
									modifier = opinion_guarding_my_food
								}
							}
						}
						
						any_courtier = {
							is_adult = yes
							in_seclusion = yes
							prisoner = no
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.1822 } # Appoint a food guard?
				}
				
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						any_courtier = {
							is_adult = yes
							in_seclusion = yes
							prisoner = no

											value < 0
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_sneaky
					}

				}
				
				# Other courtier in seclusion is kind of beautiful
				20 = {

						is_consort = no
						has_lover = no

							trait = celibate

					}

					}

					}

				# Gain a friend
				20 = {

						any_courtier = {
							at_location = ROOT
							in_seclusion = yes
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = no

								limit = { is_adult = yes }

								age >= 6

							}

				100 = {

							at_location = ROOT
							NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = RIP.12013 }

				# The Masque of the Red Death - make sure to hook in seclusion type flags!
				10 = {
					trigger = {
						is_tribal = no
						OR = {
							ai = no
							has_game_rule = {
								name = supernatural_events

								is_close_relative = ROOT
								is_pious_trigger = no
							}
						}
						
						NOR = {
							has_game_rule = {
								name = supernatural_events
								value = off
							}

							#has_character_flag = seclusion_events_on

							has_character_modifier = savoring_the_end_days

				}
				
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						is_adult = yes
						is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
						had_character_flag = { flag = seclusion_type_set days = 365 }

				250 = {

					}

				}

		else = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1817 } # Ineligible for current seclusion type - pick a new one
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_playable = yes
			}
			
			repeat_event = { id = tianxia.1820 days = 60 random = 60 } # Next flavour event
		}
		
		else = {
			repeat_event = { id = tianxia.1820 days = 100 random = 200 } # Next flavour event
		}
	}
}

# Food tombola - for food updates and minor flavour related to food
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1821
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	only_rulers = yes
	
	trigger = {
		in_seclusion = yes
	}
	
	immediate = {
		update_seclusion_food_modifier_effect = yes
		
		# Change food stores
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_temperate
			}
			
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value = -0.5
					}
				}
				80 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value = -1
					}
				}
				10 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value = -1.5
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_party
				}
			}
			
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value = -1
					}
				}
				50 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value = -1.5
					}
				}
				25 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value = -2
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value = -1
					}
				}
				20 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value = -1.5
					}
				}
				50 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value = -2
					}
				}
				20 = {
					change_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value = -2.5
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = seclusion_food
					value < -0.5
				}
			}
			
			# Ensure food always will end up positive if we get more of it somehow
			set_variable = {
				which = seclusion_food
				value = -0.5
			}
		}
		
		# Check if an alert about current food level is needed
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = {
							which = seclusion_food
							value < 10
						}
						NOR = {
							has_character_flag = seclusion_food_low
							has_character_flag = seclusion_food_very_low
							has_character_flag = seclusion_food_gone
						}
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = {
							which = seclusion_food
							value < 5
						}
						NOR = {
							has_character_flag = seclusion_food_very_low
							has_character_flag = seclusion_food_gone
						}
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = {
							which = seclusion_food
							value < 0
						}
						NOT = {
							has_character_flag = seclusion_food_gone
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value < 10
					}
					NOR = {
						has_character_flag = seclusion_food_low
						has_character_flag = seclusion_food_very_low
						has_character_flag = seclusion_food_gone
					}
				}
				
				clear_flags_with_prefix = seclusion_food_
				set_character_flag = seclusion_food_low
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2114 } # Food is getting low
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						which = seclusion_food
						value < 5
					}
					NOR = {
						has_character_flag = seclusion_food_very_low
						has_character_flag = seclusion_food_gone
					}
				}
				
				clear_flags_with_prefix = seclusion_food_
				set_character_flag = seclusion_food_very_low
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2115 } # Food is getting very low
			}
			
			else = {
				clear_flags_with_prefix = seclusion_food_
				set_character_flag = seclusion_food_gone
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2116 } # Food is effectively gone
			}
		}
		
		# Otherwise see if there should be a flavour event
		else = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						is_adult = yes
						is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
						any_courtier = {
							count = 3
							prisoner = yes
						}
						
						OR = {
							check_variable = {
								which = seclusion_food
								value < 10
							}
							trait = cruel
							trait = gluttonous
						}
						
						OR = {
							NOT = {
								has_character_flag = seclusion_prisoner_food
							}
							had_character_flag = { flag = seclusion_prisoner_food months = 6 }
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						trait = cruel
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						check_variable = {
							which = seclusion_food
							value < 5
						}
					}
					
					set_character_flag = seclusion_prisoner_food
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2117 } # Food is getting low, maybe give prisoners less?
				}
				
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						is_adult = yes
						is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
						any_courtier = {
							count = 3
							is_adult = yes
							prisoner = yes
						}
						
						trait = cannibal
						
						OR = {
							check_variable = {
								which = seclusion_food
								value < 10
							}
							trait = cruel
							trait = gluttonous
						}
						
						OR = {
							NOT = {
								has_character_flag = seclusion_cannibal_supplies
							}
							had_character_flag = { flag = seclusion_prisoner_food months = 6 }
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						trait = cruel
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						check_variable = {
							which = seclusion_food
							value < 5
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2118 } # Butcher prisoners for extra food?
				}
				
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						is_adult = yes
						is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
						any_courtier = {
							count = 3

							NOT = {

						}

# Appoint food guard?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1822
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1822
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				in_seclusion = yes
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				OR = {
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = temperate
					trait = just
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = greedy
					trait = honest
					trait = deceitful
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
					is_rival = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = food_guard_option_1
		}
		
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				in_seclusion = yes
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				NOT = {
					character = event_target:food_guard_option_1
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				OR = {
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = temperate
					trait = just
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = greedy
					trait = honest
					trait = deceitful
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
					is_rival = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = food_guard_option_2
		}
		
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				in_seclusion = yes

					trait = arbitrary

					trait = honest
					trait = deceitful

					is_lover = ROOT

				reverse_remove_opinion = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = opinion_guarding_my_food
				}
			}
		}
		
		event_target:food_guard_option_1 = {
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_guarding_my_food
			}
		}

			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {

					trait = trusting
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:food_guard_option_1 = {
					trait = gluttonous
				}
				NOR = {
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT

				}
			}

				factor = 0.1
				event_target:food_guard_option_1 = {
					trait = arbitrary
				}
				NOR = {
					is_friend = ROOT

				}
			}
		}
	}

# Food guard might decide to let certain people eat more
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1823
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1823
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1823 # Let them eat!
		
		random_list = {
			25 = {
				add_trait_partially_kind_effect = yes
			}
			25 = {
				add_trait_partially_arbitrary_effect = yes
			}
			25 = {
				add_trait_partially_deceitful_effect = yes
			}
			25 = {
				# Nothing
			}
		}
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				any_courtier = {
					limit = {
						in_seclusion = yes
						OR = {
							is_close_relative = ROOT
							is_friend = ROOT
							is_lover = ROOT
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = ROOT
								value >= 50
							}
						}
					}
					
					add_character_modifier = {
						modifier = well_fed_hidden
						months = 3
					}
					
					remove_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_not_letting_me_eat
					}
					
					opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_let_me_eat
						months = 12
					}
				}
				
				change_variable = {
					which = seclusion_food
					value = -2
				}

					50 = {
						trigger = {
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = no

								character = ROOT
								attribute = intrigue 
								value >= -5
							}
						}
						
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2

								character = ROOT
								attribute = intrigue 
								value >= 0
							}
						}

							factor = 2
							attribute_diff = {
								character = ROOT

								value >= 5
							}
						}

							factor = 2

						}

					}
					
					50 = {

								character = ROOT
								attribute = intrigue 
								value < 10
							}
						}
						
						# Not discovered
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = arbitrary
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = deceitful
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1823 # It would be wrong...
		
		random_list = {
			25 = {
				add_trait_partially_cruel_effect = yes
			}

			}

				add_trait_partially_honest_effect = yes
			}

			}

			}

# Food guard might accept bribes
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1824
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1824
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			random_courtier = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = yes
					in_seclusion = yes
					prisoner = no
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					NOR = {
						character = ROOT
						trait = temperate
					}
					wealth >= -10 # Going into a little debt is fine!
				}
				
				preferred_limit = {
					trait = gluttonous
				}
				
				save_event_target_as = bribing_courtier
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1824 # Deal!
		
		wealth = 25
		
		random_list = {
			25 = {
				add_trait_partially_greedy_effect = yes
			}
			25 = {
				add_trait_partially_arbitrary_effect = yes
			}
			25 = {
				add_trait_partially_deceitful_effect = yes
			}
			25 = {
				# Nothing
			}
		}
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:bribing_courtier = {
				wealth = -25
				
			}
			FROM = {
				any_courtier = {
					limit = {
						in_seclusion = yes
						OR = {
							is_close_relative = event_target:bribing_courtier
							is_friend = event_target:bribing_courtier
							is_lover = event_target:bribing_courtier
							reverse_opinion = {
								who = event_target:bribing_courtier
								value >= 50
							}
							character = event_target:bribing_courtier
						}
						NOT = {
							character = ROOT
						}

							factor = 2
							attribute_diff = {
								character = ROOT
								attribute = intrigue 
								value >= 0
							}
						}

							factor = 2
							attribute_diff = {
								character = ROOT
								attribute = intrigue 
								value >= 5
							}
						}

			}

			}

				who = ROOT

				months = 12
			}
		}

character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1826
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1826
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1826 # I can let it slide...
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			random = {
				chance = 25
				add_trait_partially_arbitrary_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		prestige = -50
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_rival = FROM
					trait = just
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1826 # I need a new food guard...
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
				e_china = {
					holder_scope = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						character = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				detract_grace_medium_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		remove_opinion = {
			who = FROM
			modifier = opinion_guarding_my_food
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1826 # Guards! Throw them in a cell!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
				e_china = {
					holder_scope = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						character = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				detract_grace_medium_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		remove_opinion = {
			who = FROM
			modifier = opinion_guarding_my_food
		}
		
		opinion = {
			who = FROM
			modifier = opinion_food_thief
			months = 60
		}
		
		FROM = {
			imprison = ROOT
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_friend = FROM
					is_lover = FROM
					AND = {
						is_close_relative = FROM
						NOT = {
							is_rival = FROM
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_rival = FROM
			}
		}
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1835
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1835 # I saw you!
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			add_trait_partially_paranoid_effect = yes
		}
		
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = 11 } # Consequences of discovery
			clear_event_target = target_victim
		
			pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		}
		
		FROMfROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1836 } # Inform host
		}
	}
}

# Inform ruler - food guard survived attack, known attacker
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1836
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1836
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1836 # I can let it slide...
		
		prestige = -50
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			add_trait_partially_arbitrary_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_rival = FROMFROM
					is_friend = FROM
					is_lover = FROM
					is_close_relative = FROM
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = just
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1836 # Guards! Throw them in a cell!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:food_thief
			modifier = opinion_food_thief
			months = 60
		}
		
		FROMFROM = {
			imprison = ROOT
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_friend = FROMFROM
					is_lover = FROMFROM
					AND = {
						is_close_relative = FROMFROM
						NOT = {
							is_rival = FROMFROM
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = arbitrary
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_rival = FROM
			}
		}
	}
}

			killer = FROM
		}
		
		FROMfROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1838 } # Inform host
		}
	}
}

### Prayer events

# Non-co-religionist gets inspired to convert by prayers
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1841
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1841
	picture = GFX_evt_religious_exultation
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1841 # Excellent!
		
		religion_scope = {
			save_event_target_as = target_religion
		}
		
		event_target:converting_courtier = {
			convert_religion_failsafe_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Non-co-religionist gets inspired to pray by prayers
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1842
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1842
	picture = GFX_evt_secluded_in_prayer
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1842 # I will allow it...
		
		piety = -25
		
		event_target:praying_courtier = {
			random = {
				chance = 25
				add_sympathy_for_religion_effect = yes
			}
			
			opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_let_me_pray
				months = 12
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = kind
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1842 # How can I be sure what they are praying for?
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			random = {
				chance = 25
				add_trait_partially_paranoid_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = trusting
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = paranoid
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			trait = zealous
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1842 # Stop that at once!
		
		event_target:praying_courtier = {
			opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_did_not_let_me_pray
				months = 12
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_friend = event_target:praying_courtier
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_lover = event_target:praying_courtier
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = just
			}
		}
	}
}

# Contemplaying heresy or apostasy due to ineffective prayers
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1848
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1848
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		event_target:conversion_target = {
			religion_scope = {
				save_event_target_as = target_religion
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1848 # Of course!
		
		convert_religion_failsafe_effect = yes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					has_chinese_government_trigger = no
					event_target:conversion_target = {
						could_have_chinese_government_trigger = yes
						OR = {
							NOT = {
								religion = buddhist
							}
							ROOT = {
								has_chinese_or_historical_culture_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				OR = {
					has_japanese_government_trigger = no
					event_target:conversion_target = {
						could_have_japanese_government_trigger = yes
						OR = {
							NOT = {
								religion = buddhist
							}
							ROOT = {
								culture_group = japonic
							}
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:conversion_target = {
					OR = {
						is_unreformed_pagan_trigger = no
						ROOT = {
							is_unreformed_pagan_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				NOR = {
					religion_group = event_target:conversion_target
					event_target:conversion_target = {
						ROOT = {
							has_sympathy_for_public_religion_of_prev_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				independent = no
				liege = {
					religion = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_rival = event_target:conversion_target
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				liege = {
					religion = event_target:conversion_target
					NOT = {
						is_rival = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				independent = no
				trait = paranoid
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				any_close_relative = {
					religion = ROOT
					controls_religion = yes
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				any_dynasty_member = {
					religion = ROOT
					controls_religion = yes
					NOT = {
						is_rival = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1848 # In secret, perhaps...
		
		set_secret_religion = event_target:conversion_target
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						has_chinese_government_trigger = yes
					}
					
					event_target:conversion_target = {
						OR = {
							can_have_confucian_bureaucracy_and_eastern_imperial_only_religion_trigger = no
							AND = {
								religion = buddhist
								ROOT = {
									has_chinese_or_historical_culture_trigger = no
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				trigger_else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_japanese_government_trigger = yes
					}
					
					event_target:conversion_target = {
						OR = {
							can_have_japanese_bureaucracy_and_feudal_only_religion_trigger = no
							AND = {
								religion = buddhist
								ROOT = {
									NOT = {
										culture_group = japonic
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				trigger_else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_dharmic_religion_trigger = yes
					}
					
					event_target:conversion_target = {
						has_dharmic_religion_trigger = no
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				independent = yes
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = honest
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				any_close_relative = {
					religion = ROOT
					controls_religion = yes
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				any_dynasty_member = {
					religion = ROOT
					controls_religion = yes
					NOT = {
						is_rival = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1848 # I don't think so!
		
		piety = 25
		
		random = {
			chance = 10
			add_trait_partially_zealous_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = proud
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = content
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = stubborn
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOR = {
					is_friend = event_target:conversion_target
					is_lover = event_target:conversion_target
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Zealous unbeliever tries to convert praying character
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1849
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1849
	picture = GFX_evt_emissary
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		event_target:conversion_target = {
			religion_scope = {
				save_event_target_as = target_religion
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			controls_religion = no
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1849 # Of course!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_secret_religion = yes
				NOT = {
					true_religion = event_target:conversion_target
				}
			}
			
			clear_secret_religion = yes
		}
		
		convert_religion_failsafe_effect = yes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					has_chinese_government_trigger = no
					event_target:conversion_target = {
						could_have_chinese_government_trigger = yes
						OR = {
							NOT = {
								religion = buddhist
							}
							ROOT = {
								has_chinese_or_historical_culture_trigger = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
				OR = {
					has_japanese_government_trigger = no
					event_target:conversion_target = {
						could_have_japanese_government_trigger = yes
						OR = {
							NOT = {
								religion = buddhist
							}
							ROOT = {
								culture_group = japonic
							}
						}
					}
				}
				event_target:conversion_target = {
					OR = {
						is_unreformed_pagan_trigger = no
						ROOT = {
							is_unreformed_pagan_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					trait = zealous
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				NOR = {
					religion_group = event_target:conversion_target
					event_target:conversion_target = {
						ROOT = {
							has_sympathy_for_public_religion_of_prev_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				independent = no
				liege = {
					religion = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_rival = event_target:conversion_target
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				liege = {
					religion = event_target:conversion_target
					NOR = {
						is_rival = ROOT
						ROOT = {
							same_secret_religion_as_prev_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				independent = no
				trait = paranoid
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				any_close_relative = {
					religion = ROOT
					controls_religion = yes
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				any_dynasty_member = {
					religion = ROOT
					controls_religion = yes
					NOT = {
						is_rival = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			controls_religion = no
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1849 # In secret, perhaps...
		
		set_secret_religion = event_target:conversion_target
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				trigger_if = {
					limit = {
						has_chinese_government_trigger = yes
					}
					
					event_target:conversion_target = {
						OR = {
							can_have_confucian_bureaucracy_and_eastern_imperial_only_religion_trigger = no
							AND = {
								religion = buddhist
								ROOT = {
									has_chinese_or_historical_culture_trigger = no
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				trigger_else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_japanese_government_trigger = yes
					}
					
					event_target:conversion_target = {
						OR = {
							can_have_japanese_bureaucracy_and_feudal_only_religion_trigger = no
							AND = {
								religion = buddhist
								ROOT = {
									NOT = {
										culture_group = japonic
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				trigger_else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_dharmic_religion_trigger = yes
					}
					
					event_target:conversion_target = {
						has_dharmic_religion_trigger = no
					}
				}
				
				NOT = {
					trait = zealous
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				independent = yes
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = honest
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				any_close_relative = {
					religion = ROOT
					controls_religion = yes
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				any_dynasty_member = {
					religion = ROOT
					controls_religion = yes
					NOT = {
						is_rival = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1849 # I don't think so!
		
		piety = 25
		
		random = {
			chance = 10
			add_trait_partially_zealous_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = proud
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = content
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = stubborn
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				NOR = {
					is_friend = event_target:conversion_target
					is_lover = event_target:conversion_target
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			trait = zealous
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_1849 # Guards! Imprison them!
		
		piety = 50
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_friend = event_target:conversion_target
					is_lover = event_target:conversion_target
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = wroth
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_rival = event_target:conversion_target
			}
		}
	}
}

# Courtier makes a friend at the party
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1851
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1851
	picture = GFX_evt_dancing
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
				is_lover = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
			}
			OR = {
				is_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
				is_lover = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
			}
			NOR = {
				is_rival = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
				is_rival = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1851 # Great!
		
		event_target:friendly_courtier_1 = {
			add_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NAND = {
				OR = {
					is_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
					is_lover = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
				}
				OR = {
					is_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
					is_lover = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
				}
			}
			NOR = {
				is_rival = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
				is_rival = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1851 # Good for them!
		
		event_target:friendly_courtier_1 = {
			add_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
				is_lover = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
			}
			is_rival = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1851 # A strange choice...
		
		event_target:friendly_courtier_1 = {
			add_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
		}
	}

		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				is_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
				is_lover = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
				is_rival = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
			}
			is_rival = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_1851 # I see...
		
		event_target:friendly_courtier_1 = {
			add_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_rival = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
			OR = {
				is_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
				is_lover = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTE_TIANXIA_1851 # A strange choice...
		
		event_target:friendly_courtier_1 = {
			add_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_rival = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
			NOR = {
				is_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
				is_lover = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
				is_rival = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTF_TIANXIA_1851 # I see...
		
		event_target:friendly_courtier_1 = {
			add_friend = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_rival = event_target:friendly_courtier_1
			is_rival = event_target:friendly_courtier_2
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTG_TIANXIA_1851 # They might be conspiring against me!

			chance = 25
			add_trait_partially_paranoid_effect = yes
		}

		}
	}

# Met someone pretty at the party - attempt to tumble?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1854
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1854
	picture = GFX_evt_dancing
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1854 # Yes!
		
		remove_trait_chaste_effect = yes
		
		event_target:seclusion_lover = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1855 days = 1 } # Other party approached
		}
		
		piety = -10
		
		ai_chance = {
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = chaste
					trait = celibate
					AND = {
						is_ascetic_trigger = yes
						NOT = {
							trait = lustful
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:seclusion_lover = {
					is_unattractive_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 5
				event_target:seclusion_lover = {
					is_attractive_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1854 # I don't think so!
		
		piety = 10
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			remove_trait_lustful_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = lustful
					trait = master_seducer
					trait = master_seductress
				}
			}
			
			factor = 100
		}
	}
}
