	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1871
	picture = GFX_evt_dancing
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1871 # I must look my best!
		
		add_trait_partially_groomed_effect = yes
	}
}

# Peasants start getting suspicious
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1872
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1872
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1872 # Nothing to see here!
		
		add_trait_partially_deceitful_effect = yes
		
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					intrigue >= 10
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					intrigue >= 20
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					intrigue >= 30
				}
				
				# Nothing
			}
			
			50 = {
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					intrigue < 5
				}
				
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = peasants_get_suspicious
					set_character_flag = seclusion_peasant_supicion
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = honest
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = deceitful
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1872 # It's fine!
		
		prestige = 25
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = peasants_get_suspicious
			set_character_flag = seclusion_peasant_supicion
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = deceitful
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = honest
			}
		}
	}
}

# Peasants restless
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1873
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1873
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1873 # That's not good...
		
		set_character_flag = seclusion_peasant_party_unrest
		
		capital_scope = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = peasants_upset
				years = 2
			}
		}
	}
}

# Peasants attempt to break in
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1874
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1874
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1874 # Drive them back!
		
		set_character_flag = seclusion_peasant_party_unrest
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						martial >= 10
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						martial >= 15
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						martial >= 20
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						martial >= 25
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						martial >= 30
					}
				
					character_event = { id = tianxia.1875 days = 3 random = 3 } # Peasants driven back
				}
			
				50 = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.1876 days = 3 random = 3 } # Peasants get inside
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Peasants driven back
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1875
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1875
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1875 # And stay out!
		
		prestige = 100
		
		random = {
			chance = 50
			add_trait_partially_proud_effect = yes
		}
		
		capital_scope = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = peasants_upset
				years = 2
			}
		}
	}
}

# The peasants got in - fight for your life!
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1876
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1876
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				capital_scope = {
					has_disease = yes
				}
			}
			
			random = {
				chance = 50
				set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
			}
			
			any_courtier = {
				limit = {
					is_adult = yes
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					in_seclusion = yes
					prisoner = no
				}
				
				random = {
					chance = 50
					set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1876 # To arms!
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						combat_rating < 50
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						combat_rating < 25
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						combat_rating < 0
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.1877 days = 2 } # Death
				}
				
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						combat_rating < 75
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.1880 days = 2 } # Maimed
				}
				
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						combat_rating < 100
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.1881 days = 2 } # Wounded
				}
				
				25 = {
					trigger = {
						combat_rating >= 50
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.1882 days = 2 } # Wounded
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Death outcome - check if it is safe for the peasants to take over the capital
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1877
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				higher_tier_than = baron
				OR = {
					ai = yes
					any_demesne_title = {
						count >= 2
						tier = count
						holding_is_safe_for_roots_gov_trigger = yes
					}
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1878 } # Capital takeover and death
		}
		
		else = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1879 } # Death
		}
	}
}

# Peasants kill you, take over your capital, and might kill/maim/wound your courtiers
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1878
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1878
	picture = GFX_evt_bloody_man
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		capital_scope = {
			save_event_target_as = capital_county
		}
		
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					OR = {
						has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
						has_religion_feature = religion_equal
						has_religion_feature = religion_feature_bon
						has_religion_feature = religion_feature_bai
						has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minahasan
						has_religion_feature = religion_feature_ryukyuan
						has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
					}
				}
				create_random_soldier = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = random
					female = yes
					religion = ROOT
					culture = ROOT
					race = ROOT
					health = 5
					age = 25
					add_trait = peasant_leader
					add_trait = ambitious
					add_trait = gregarious
					add_trait = wroth
					add_trait = cruel
				}
			}
			
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					NOR = {
						has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
						has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
					}
				}
				create_random_soldier = {
					random_traits = yes
					dynasty = random
					female = no
					religion = ROOT
					culture = ROOT
					race = ROOT
					health = 5
					age = 25
					add_trait = peasant_leader
					add_trait = ambitious
					add_trait = gregarious
					add_trait = wroth
					add_trait = cruel
				}
			}
		}
		
		new_character = {
			save_event_target_as = peasant_commander
			
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
		}
		
		player_heir = {
			save_event_target_as = my_heir
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1878 # This cannot be!
			
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:peasant_commander
			modifier = opinion_rebel_traitor
		}
			
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:peasant_commander
			modifier = opinion_regicide_traitor
		}
		
		event_target:capital_county = {
			county = {
				usurp_title = event_target:peasant_commander
				
				hidden_tooltip = {
					add_claim = event_target:my_heir
				}
			}
		}
		
		event_target:peasant_commander = {
			set_defacto_liege = THIS

			spawn_unit = {
				province = event_target:capital_county
				home = event_target:capital_county
				owner = THIS
				scaled_by_biggest_garrison = 1.75
				attrition = 1.0

				troops = {
					archers = { 6 6 }
					light_cavalry = { 3 3 }
					heavy_infantry = { 1 1 }
					light_infantry = { 7 7 }
					pikemen = { 3 3 }
				}
			}
		}
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = peasants_go_on_a_rampage
			
			any_courtier = {
				limit = {
					prisoner = yes
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_adult = yes
						is_lowborn = no
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
							e_china = {
								holder_scope = {
									is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
									OR = {
										character = PREVPREV
										AND = {
											is_close_relative = PREVPREV
											dynasty = PREVPREV
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						
						if = {
							limit = {
								is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
							}
							event_target:peasant_commander = {
								detract_grace_super_huge_effect = yes
							}
						}
						else = {
							event_target:peasant_commander = {
								detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
							}
						}
					}
					
					death = {
						death_reason = death_execution_torn_apart_by_a_mob
						killer = event_target:peasant_commander
					}
					
					event_target:peasant_commander = {
						pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
					}
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_lowborn = yes
					}
					
					imprison = event_target:peasant_commander
				}
				
				else = {
					force_host = event_target:peasant_commander
				}
			}
			
			any_courtier = {
				limit = {
					in_seclusion = yes
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_adult = yes
						is_lowborn = no
					}
					
					random_list = {
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								OR = {
									NOR = {
										combat_rating >= 50
										intrigue >= 5
									}
									is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
								}
							}
							
							mult_modifier = {
								factor = 0.1
								is_heir = yes
							}
							
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
									e_china = {
										holder_scope = {
											is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
											OR = {
												character = PREVPREV
												AND = {
													is_close_relative = PREVPREV
													dynasty = PREVPREV
												}
											}
										}
									}
								}
								
								if = {
									limit = {
										is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
									}
									event_target:peasant_commander = {
										detract_grace_super_huge_effect = yes
									}
								}
								else = {
									event_target:peasant_commander = {
										detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
									}
								}
							}
							
							death = {
								death_reason = death_execution_torn_apart_by_a_mob
								killer = event_target:peasant_commander
							}
							
							event_target:peasant_commander = {
								pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
							}
						}
						
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									combat_rating >= 75
									intrigue >= 10
								}
							}
							
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
									e_china = {
										holder_scope = {
											is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
											OR = {
												character = PREVPREV
												AND = {
													is_close_relative = PREVPREV
													dynasty = PREVPREV
												}
											}
										}
									}
								}
								
								if = {
									limit = {
										is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
									}
									event_target:peasant_commander = {
										detract_grace_super_huge_effect = yes
									}
								}
								else = {
									event_target:peasant_commander = {
										detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
									}
								}
							}
							
							add_maimed_trait_effect = yes
						}
						
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									combat_rating >= 100
									intrigue >= 15
								}
							}
							
							add_trait = wounded
						}
						
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							}
							
							# Survives unharmed
						}
					}
				}
					
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_lowborn = no
					}
					
					random_list = {
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								OR = {
									NOR = {
										combat_rating >= 50
										intrigue >= 5
									}
									is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
								}
								is_heir = no
							}
				
							imprison = event_target:peasant_commander
						}
						
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									combat_rating >= 100
									intrigue >= 15
								}
								is_heir = no
							}
							
							add_trait = wounded
						}
						
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								OR = {
									is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
									is_heir = yes
								}
							}
							
							# Escaped unharmed
						}
					}
				}
				
				else = {
					force_host = event_target:peasant_commander
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
				e_china = {
					holder_scope = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						OR = {
							character = PREVPREV
							AND = {
								is_close_relative = PREVPREV
								dynasty = PREVPREV
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
				}
				event_target:peasant_commander = {
					detract_grace_super_huge_effect = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				event_target:peasant_commander = {
					detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		death = {
			death_reason = death_execution_torn_apart_by_a_mob
			killer = event_target:peasant_commander
		}
		
		event_target:peasant_commander = {
			pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Peasants kill you and might kill/maim/wound your courtiers
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1879
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1879
	picture = GFX_evt_bloody_man
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1879 # This cannot be!
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = peasants_go_on_a_rampage
			
			any_courtier = {
				limit = {
					in_seclusion = yes
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_adult = yes
					}
					
					random_list = {
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								OR = {
									is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
									NOR = {
										combat_rating >= 50
										intrigue >= 5
									}
								}
							}
							
							mult_modifier = {
								factor = 0.1
								is_heir = yes
							}
							
							death = {
								death_reason = death_rabble
							}
						}
						
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									combat_rating >= 75
									intrigue >= 10
								}
							}
							
							add_maimed_trait_effect = yes
						}
						
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									combat_rating >= 100
									intrigue >= 15
								}
							}
							
							add_trait = wounded
						}
						
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							}
							
							# Survives unharmed
						}
					}
				}
				
				else = {
					random_list = {
						75 = {
							trigger = {
								OR = {
									is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
									NOR = {
										combat_rating >= 100
										intrigue >= 15
									}
								}
							}
							
							add_trait = wounded
						}
						
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							}
							
							# Escaped unharmed
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		death = {
			death_reason = death_rabble
		}
	}
}

# Peasants maim you and might maim/wound your courtiers
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1880
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1880
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				trait = cannibal
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1880 # Curses!

								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {

									intrigue >= 15

									is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
									NOR = {

										intrigue >= 15

								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							}
							
							# Escaped unharmed
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			trait = cannibal
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1880 # At least there's fresh meat...
		
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_cannibal_supplies
		set_character_flag = seclusion_cannibal_supplies
		
		add_maimed_trait_effect = yes
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			change_variable = {
				which = seclusion_food
				value = 3
			}
		}
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = minor_peasant_rampage
			
			any_courtier = {
				limit = {
					in_seclusion = yes
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_adult = yes
					}
					
					random_list = {
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								OR = {
									is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
									NOR = {
										combat_rating >= 75
										intrigue >= 10
									}
								}
							}
							
							add_maimed_trait_effect = yes
						}
						
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									combat_rating >= 100
									intrigue >= 15
								}
							}
							
							add_trait = wounded
						}
						
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							}
							
							# Survives unharmed
						}
					}
				}
					
				else = {
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								OR = {
									is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
									NOR = {
										combat_rating >= 100
										intrigue >= 15
									}
								}
							}
							
							add_trait = wounded
						}
						
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							}
							
							# Escaped unharmed
						}
					}

	}

# Peasants wound you and might wound your courtiers
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1881
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1881
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				trait = cannibal
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1881 # Not great...
		
		add_trait_wounded_effect = yes
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = minor_peasant_rampage
			
			any_courtier = {
				limit = {
					in_seclusion = yes
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_adult = yes
					}
					
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								OR = {
									is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
									NOR = {
										combat_rating >= 100
										intrigue >= 15
									}
								}
							}
							
							add_trait = wounded
						}
						
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							}
							
							# Survives unharmed
						}
					}
				}
				
				else = {
					random_list = {
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								OR = {
									is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
									NOR = {
										combat_rating >= 100
										intrigue >= 15
									}
								}
							}
							
							add_trait = wounded
						}
						
						75 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							}
							
							# Escaped unharmed
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			trait = cannibal
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1881 # We got some food from it, at least...
		
		add_trait_wounded_effect = yes
		
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_cannibal_supplies
		set_character_flag = seclusion_cannibal_supplies
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			change_variable = {
				which = seclusion_food
				value = 3
			}
		}
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = minor_peasant_rampage
			
			any_courtier = {
				limit = {
					in_seclusion = yes
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_adult = yes
					}
					
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									combat_rating >= 100
									intrigue >= 15
								}
							}
							
							add_trait = wounded
						}
						
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							}
							
							# Survives unharmed
						}
					}
				}
				
				else = {
					random_list = {
						25 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
								NOR = {
									combat_rating >= 100
									intrigue >= 15
								}
							}
							
							add_trait = wounded
						}
						
						75 = {
							trigger = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							}
							
							# Escaped unharmed
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

# Discovered promising way to gain more taxes - possibly gain Greedy
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1888
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1888
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1888 # Double the taxes! Triple the taxes!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					trait = greedy
				}
			}
			
			add_trait_partially_greedy_effect = yes
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NAND = {
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = cruel
				}
			}
			
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					add_trait_partially_arbitrary_effect = yes
				}
				25 = {
					add_trait_partially_cruel_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		piety = -25
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = charitable
					NOR = {
						trait = arbitrary
						trait = cruel
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = greedy
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = arbitrary
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1888 # It would be wrong...
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				trait = greedy
			}
			
			remove_trait_greedy_effect = yes
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NAND = {
					trait = just
					trait = kind
				}
			}
			
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					add_trait_partially_kind_effect = yes
				}
				25 = {
					add_trait_partially_just_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = greedy
					NOR = {
						trait = just
						trait = kind
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = charitable
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = just
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = kind
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = tianxia.1908
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1908
	picture = GFX_evt_relaxed_ruler
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1908 # I don't need to eat a lot!
		
		add_trait_partially_temperate_effect = yes
	}
}

### Gardening events

# Slothful courtier is not happy
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1910
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1910
	picture = GFX_evt_poke_king_in_chest
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1910 # You can't please everyone...
		
		event_target:annoyed_courtier = {
			opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_bad_seclusion_type
			}
		}
	}
}

### Sneaky events

# Courtier gets annoyed by you skulking about
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1916
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1916
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				trait = paranoid
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1916 # You can't please everyone...
		
		event_target:annoyed_courtier = {
			opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_bad_seclusion_type
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			trait = paranoid
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1916 # What is he/she up to?
		
		event_target:annoyed_courtier = {
			opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_bad_seclusion_type
			}
			
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_suspect_courtier
				months = 12
			}
		}
	}
}

# Someone is sneaking about - follow them?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1917
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1917
	picture = GFX_evt_hiding_from_pursuers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1917 # I will follow them!
		
		character_event = { id = tianxia.1918 days = 2 random = 5 } # Determine outcome
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NAND = {
				trait = paranoid
				NOR = {
					trait = content
					trait = slothful
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1917 # It is probably nothing...
			
		random = {
			chance = 10
			add_trait_partially_trusting_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = paranoid
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Determine what happens
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1918
	hide_window = yes
	
	only_playable = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
		event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
			is_alive = yes
			is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
			prisoner = no
			host = {
				character = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
						any_lover = {
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							prisoner = no
							OR = {
								in_seclusion = yes
								ROOT = {
									in_seclusion = no
								}
							}
							host = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							NOR = {
								is_married = PREV
								is_consort = PREV
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:sneaky_courtier
						attribute = intrigue
						value >= 0
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
						OR = {
							is_married = ROOT
							is_consort = ROOT
							is_close_relative = ROOT
							any_lover = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
								prisoner = no
								OR = {
									in_seclusion = yes
									ROOT = {
										in_seclusion = no
									}
								}
								host = {
									character = ROOT
								}
								NOR = {
									is_married = PREV
									is_consort = PREV
									character = ROOT
								}
								OR = {
									is_married = ROOT
									is_consort = ROOT
									is_close_relative = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:sneaky_courtier
						attribute = intrigue
						value >= 5
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:sneaky_courtier
						attribute = intrigue
						value >= 10
					}
				}
				
				event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
					random_lover = {
						limit = {
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							prisoner = no
							OR = {
								in_seclusion = yes
								ROOT = {
									in_seclusion = no
								}
							}
							host = {
								character = ROOT
							}
							NOR = {
								is_married = PREV
								is_consort = PREV
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
						
						preferred_limit = {
							OR = {
								is_married = ROOT
								is_consort = ROOT
								is_close_relative = ROOT
							}
						}
						
						save_event_target_as = discovered_lover
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.1919 } # Discovered sneaky character meeting up with lover
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
						has_secret_religion = yes
						society_member_of = secret_religious_cult
						NAND = {
							true_religion = ROOT
							ROOT = {
								true_religion = PREV
							}
						}
					}
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:sneaky_courtier
						attribute = intrigue
						value >= 0
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:sneaky_courtier
						attribute = intrigue
						value >= 5
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:sneaky_courtier
						attribute = intrigue
						value >= 10
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.1920 } # Discovered sneaky character attending SRS meeting
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
						is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
						NOT = {

						}
					}

					}

			25 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
						OR = {
							has_plot = plot_kill_character
							has_plot = plot_kill_spouse

							is_actually_incapable_trigger = no

						value >= 0
					}
					
					NOR = {
						trait = craven
						is_maimed_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:sneaky_courtier
						attribute = intrigue
						value >= 5
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					attribute_diff = {
						character = event_target:sneaky_courtier

						value >= 10
					}
				}
				
				event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
					plot_target_char = {
						save_event_target_as = plot_target
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.1924 } # Discovered sneaky character about to murder someone
			}

			}
			
			25 = {

# Discovered that they have a lover at court
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1919
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1919
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		# Adultery fallout
		event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
			save_event_target_as = target_adulterer
		}
		
		event_target:discovered_lover = {
			character_event = { id = 20 }
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				is_married = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				is_consort = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				is_married = event_target:discovered_lover
				is_consort = event_target:discovered_lover
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1919 # This has to end!

		event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
			show_scope_change = no
			
			remove_lover = event_target:discovered_lover

			opinion = {
				name = opinion_angry
				who = ROOT
				years = 5
			}
		}

		reverse_opinion = {
			name = opinion_angry
			who = event_target:discovered_lover
			years = 5
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				OR = {
					is_close_relative = event_target:sneaky_courtier
					is_close_relative = event_target:discovered_lover
				}
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_rival = event_target:sneaky_courtier
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_rival = event_target:discovered_lover
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
				can_marry = event_target:discovered_lover
			}
			NOR = {
				is_married = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				is_consort = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				is_married = event_target:discovered_lover
				is_consort = event_target:discovered_lover
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1919 # I suppose they need to get married...

		event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
			show_scope_change = no
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
					dynasty = ROOT
					ROOT = {
						NOR = {
							has_religion_feature = religion_patriarchal
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_batak
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_yi
							AND = {
								OR = {
									religion_group = muslim
									religion = confucian
								}
								has_law = agnatic_succession
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				add_spouse_matrilineal = event_target:discovered_lover
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = no
					dynasty = ROOT
					ROOT = {
						NOR = {
							has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
						}
					}
				}
				
				add_spouse = event_target:discovered_lover
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						OR = {
							has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
						}
					}
				}
				
				add_spouse_matrilineal = event_target:discovered_lover
			}
			
			else = {
				add_spouse = event_target:discovered_lover
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				has_religion_feature = religion_dogmatic
				has_secret_religion = no
				OR = {
					religion = event_target:sneaky_courtier
					religion = event_target:discovered_lover
				}
				NAND = {
					religion = event_target:sneaky_courtier
					religion = event_target:discovered_lover
				}
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = zealous
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = proud
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = chaste
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				is_married = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				is_consort = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				is_married = event_target:discovered_lover
				is_consort = event_target:discovered_lover
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1919 # I will leave them be...
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_close_relative = event_target:sneaky_courtier
					is_close_relative = event_target:discovered_lover
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = lustful
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				is_married = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				is_consort = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				is_married = event_target:discovered_lover
				is_consort = event_target:discovered_lover
				is_close_relative = event_target:discovered_lover
			}
			is_close_relative = event_target:sneaky_courtier
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_1919 # Guards! Seize that person!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_rival = event_target:discovered_lover
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = cruel
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				is_married = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				is_consort = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				is_married = event_target:discovered_lover
				is_consort = event_target:discovered_lover
				is_close_relative = event_target:sneaky_courtier
			}
			is_close_relative = event_target:discovered_lover
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTE_TIANXIA_1919 # Guards! Seize that person!
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_rival = event_target:sneaky_courtier
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = cruel
			}
		}
	}

# Discovered them attending an SRS meeting
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1920
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1920
	picture = GFX_evt_secret_ceremony
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					host = {
						character = ROOT
					}
					prisoner = no
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					OR = {
						in_seclusion = yes
						ROOT = {
							in_seclusion = no
						}
					}
				}
				
				increase_visibility_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	# Largely reuses MNM.70200's setup

	option = { # Execute them!
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				tolerates_other_religions_trigger = no
				event_target:accused_apostate = {
					is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
						has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
					}
				}
			}
		 }
		 
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1920

		piety = 10

		tooltip = {
			event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
				show_scope_change = no
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
						e_china = {
							holder_scope = {
								is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
								OR = {
									character = event_target:sneaky_courtier
									AND = {
										is_close_relative = event_target:sneaky_courtier
										dynasty = event_target:sneaky_courtier
									}
								}
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						}
						ROOT = {
							detract_grace_super_huge_effect = yes
						}
					}
					else = {
						ROOT = {
							detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
						}
					}
				}

				death = {
					death_reason = death_execution_burning
					killer = ROOT
				}
			}
		}

		event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
			save_event_target_as = kinslayer_target
		}

		add_kinslayer_trait_effect = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes

						OR = {
							has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
							has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
						}
					}

					add_character_modifier = {
						name = known_satanist
						duration = -1
					}
				}
				else = {
					add_character_modifier = {
						name = accused_apostate
						duration = -1
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						religion = ROOT
						controls_religion = no
						religion_has_excommunication = yes
					}

					add_trait = excommunicated
				}
			}

			opinion = {
				name = opinion_accused_apostate
				who = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				years = 100
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_nickname = no

					event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
						NOT = { character = event_target:innocent_victim }
					}
				}

				random_list = {
					500 = { } # No Nickname
					5 = { give_nickname = nick_the_cleansing_flame }
					5 = { give_nickname = nick_the_witch_hunter }
					5 = { give_nickname = nick_the_inquisitor }
					5 = { give_nickname = nick_the_purifier }
				}
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = lunatic
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = possessed
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = cruel
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = paranoid
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = arbitrary
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 100
				trait = zealous
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				tolerates_other_religions_trigger = yes
				event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
					NAND = {
						is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes

						OR = {
							has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
							has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Imprison them
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1920

		reverse_imprison = event_target:sneaky_courtier

		opinion = {
			name = opinion_accused_apostate
			who = event_target:sneaky_courtier
			years = 100
		}

		event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
			show_scope_change = no
				
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
					e_china = {
						holder_scope = {
							is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
							OR = {
								character = event_target:sneaky_courtier
								AND = {
									is_close_relative = event_target:sneaky_courtier
									dynasty = event_target:sneaky_courtier
								}
							}
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
					}
					ROOT = {
						detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
					}
				}
				else = {
					ROOT = {
						detract_grace_major_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes

					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
						has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
					}
				}

				add_character_modifier = {
					name = known_satanist
					duration = -1
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					name = accused_apostate
					duration = -1
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					religion = ROOT
					controls_religion = no
					religion_has_excommunication = yes
				}

				add_trait = excommunicated
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = kind
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 3
				event_target:sneaky_courtier = { ai = no }
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 5
				dynasty = event_target:sneaky_courtier
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				tolerates_other_religions_trigger = yes
				event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
					NAND = {
						is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes

						OR = {
							has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
							has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Brand them
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				tolerates_other_religions_trigger = no
				event_target:accused_apostate = {
					is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
						has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
					}
				}
			}
		 }
				
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1920

		opinion = {
			name = opinion_accused_apostate
			who = event_target:accused_apostate
			years = 100
		}

		event_target:accused_apostate = {
			show_scope_change = no
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
					e_china = {
						holder_scope = {
							is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
							OR = {
								character = event_target:accused_apostate
								AND = {
									is_close_relative = event_target:accused_apostate
									dynasty = event_target:accused_apostate
								}
							}
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
					}
					ROOT = {
						detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
					}
				}
				else = {
					ROOT = {
						detract_grace_major_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes

					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
						has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
					}
				}

				add_character_modifier = {
					name = known_satanist
					duration = -1
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					name = accused_apostate
					duration = -1
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					religion = ROOT
					controls_religion = no
					religion_has_excommunication = yes
				}

				add_trait = excommunicated
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100

			trigger = { trait = trusting }
		}
	}

	option = { # Let them go
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_1920

		piety = -10

		opinion = {
			name = opinion_apostate_timer
			who = event_target:accused_apostate
			years = 10
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			trigger = { trait = trusting }
		}
	}
}

				limit = {
					religion = ROOT
					controls_religion = no
					religion_has_excommunication = yes
				}

				add_trait = excommunicated
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100

			trigger = { trait = trusting }
		}
	}

	option = { # Let them go
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_1921

		piety = -10

		opinion = {
			name = opinion_apostate_timer
			who = event_target:accused_apostate
			years = 10
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			trigger = { trait = trusting }
		}
	}
}

						event_target:rival_to_kill = {
							attribute_diff = {
								character = ROOT
								attribute = intrigue
								value >= 5
							}
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						event_target:rival_to_kill = {
							attribute_diff = {
								character = ROOT
								attribute = intrigue
								value >= 10
							}
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.1940 days = 1 } # Failed to kill the target, discovered
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = craven
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = patient
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1936 # Too risky...
		
		prestige = -50
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = brave
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = wroth
			}
		}
	}
}

# Have time to spend time with spouse/consort - might grow closer to them/become lovers
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1947
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1947
	picture = GFX_evt_family
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			event_target:growing_closer = {
				is_valid_romance_target = ROOT
				opinion = {
					who = ROOT
					value >= 40
				}
				reverse_opinion = {
					who = ROOT
					value >= 40
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1947 # True love!
		
		add_lover = event_target:growing_closer
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				event_target:growing_closer = {
					is_valid_romance_target = ROOT
					opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 40
					}
					reverse_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value >= 40
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1947 # This is good!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:growing_closer
			modifier = opinion_seclusion_happy_with_relationship
		}
		
		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:growing_closer
			modifier = opinion_seclusion_happy_with_relationship
		}
	}
}

# Befriending own child
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1948
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1948
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_mother
		trigger = {
			event_target:growing_closer = {
				is_female = yes
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_father
		trigger = {
			event_target:growing_closer = {
				is_female = yes
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
		trigger = {
			event_target:growing_closer = {
				is_female = no
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_son_asking_father
		trigger = {
			event_target:growing_closer = {
				is_female = no
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1948 # This can only mean good things!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_rival = event_target:growing_closer
			}
			
			remove_rival = event_target:growing_closer
		}
		
		else = {
			add_friend = event_target:growing_closer
		}
	}
}

### Child, grandchild, or sibling wants to leave seclusion events - rewritten vanilla events moved here due to a more extensive rework

# Relative might ask to leave or might decide to sneak out right away
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1950
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1950
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			save_event_target_as = seclusion_host
			capital_scope = {
				save_event_target_as = seclusion_location
			}
		}
		
		clear_flags_with_prefix = seclusion_leave_reason_
		
		# Pick the actual reason to leave
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					trait = lustful
					is_ascetic_trigger = no
					NOR = {
						trait = celibate
						any_lover = {
							in_seclusion = yes
							prisoner = no
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = no

								character = event_target:seclusion_host
							}

					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					is_attractive_trigger = yes
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = hedonist
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = decadent
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = master_seducer
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = master_seductress
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.1
					trait = zealous
				}
				
				set_character_flag = seclusion_leave_reason_brothel
			}
			
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					trait = greedy
					OR = {
						trait = game_master
						trait = ambitious
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = gregarious
				}
				
				set_character_flag = seclusion_leave_reason_gambling
			}
			
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					trait = humble
					any_friend = {
						is_landed = no
						distance = {
							who = event_target:seclusion_host
							value < 200
						}
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					any_friend = {
						is_landed = no
						distance = {
							who = event_target:seclusion_host
							value < 200
						}
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = hedonist
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 10
					trait = drunkard
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = decadent
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 0.1
					trait = temperate
				}
				
				set_character_flag = seclusion_leave_reason_drinking
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							NOT = {
								same_society_as = event_target:seclusion_host
							}
							OR = {
								society_member_of = secret_religious_cult
								is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
							}
						}
						trait = greedy
						AND = {
							OR = {
								has_plot = plot_kill_character
								has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
							}
							NOT = {
								any_plot_backer = {
									character = event_target:seclusion_host
								}
							}
						}
						society_member_of = the_alchemists
						society_member_of = hermetics
						is_monastic_member_trigger = yes
						trait = deceitful
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 5
					NOT = {
						same_society_as = event_target:seclusion_host
					}
					OR = {
						society_member_of = secret_religious_cult
						is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 5
					OR = {
						has_plot = plot_kill_character
						has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
					}
					NOT = {
						any_plot_backer = {
							character = event_target:seclusion_host
						}
					}
				}
				
				set_character_flag = seclusion_leave_reason_meeting
			}
			
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					is_dumb_trigger = yes
					NOR = {
						trait = craven
						trait = paranoid
					}
				}
				
				set_character_flag = seclusion_leave_reason_walk
			}
			
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					OR = {
						trait = hunter
						AND = {
							trait = brave
							has_education_martial_trigger = yes
							can_be_commander_no_fromfrom_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					NOR = {
						trait = craven
						trait = paranoid
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 5
					trait = hunter
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					event_target:seclusion_host = {
						check_variable = {
							which = seclusion_food
							value < 5
						}
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					event_target:seclusion_host = {
						check_variable = {
							which = seclusion_food
							value < 0
						}
					}
				}
				
				set_character_flag = seclusion_leave_reason_hunt
			}
			
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						trait = craven
					}
					OR = {
						AND = {
							OR = {
								is_member_of_any_warrior_lodge_trigger = yes
								society_member_of = warriors_of_the_rising_sun
							}
							OR = {
								any_society_member = {
									is_adult = yes
									same_society_as = PREV
									is_landed = no
									distance = {
										who = event_target:seclusion_host
										value < 200
									}
									is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								}
								any_rival = {
									is_adult = yes
									is_landed = no
									distance = {
										who = event_target:seclusion_host
										value < 200
									}
									is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
								}
							}
						}
						trait = duelist
						AND = {
							trait = brave
							any_rival = {
								is_adult = yes
								is_landed = no
								distance = {
									who = event_target:seclusion_host
									value < 200
								}
								trait = duelist
								is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 5
					trait = duelist
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					OR = {
						is_member_of_any_warrior_lodge_trigger = yes
						society_member_of = warriors_of_the_rising_sun
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = brave
				}
				
				set_character_flag = seclusion_leave_reason_duel
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1950 # I will sneak out!
		
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					intrigue >= 10
					attribute_diff = {
						character = FROM
						attribute = intrigue
						value >= 0
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					attribute_diff = {
						character = FROM
						attribute = intrigue
						value >= 5
					}
				}

					factor = 2
					attribute_diff = {

						attribute = intrigue
						value >= 10
					}
				}

			}
			
			50 = {

					FROM = {
						is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
						is_adult = yes
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = FROM
						modifier = opinion_suspect_courtier
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					FROM = {
						has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_sneaky
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.1951 } # Liege notices attempt to leave
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = deceitful
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = arbitrary
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {

			FROM = {
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1950 # I will just ask...

# Relative caught trying to sneak out - prevent them from leaving or have them tailed?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1951
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1951
	picture = GFX_evt_hiding_from_pursuers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1951 # I trust them!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				trait = paranoid
			}
			
			remove_trait = paranoid
		}
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1959 } # Relative sneaked out, if not very stealthily
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = paranoid
					AND = {
						is_rival = FROM
						NOT = {
							trait = trusting
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = trusting
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1951 # I'll have them tailed...
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				trait = trusting
			}
			
			remove_trait = trusting
		}
		
		FROM = {
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_tailed_by_agents
				months = 1
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1959 } # Relative sneaked out, tailed by agents
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = honest
					NOT = {
						is_rival = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = honest
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = trusting
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1951 # They have some explaining to do!
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1962 } # Relative confronted about trying to leave
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = trusting
					NOT = {
						is_rival = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = deceitful
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = paranoid
			}
		}
	}
}

			has_character_flag = seclusion_leave_excuse_brothel
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1962 # I am heading to the brothel (not really)!
		
		event_target:seclusion_host = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1964 days = 2 } # Inform host
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					AND = {
						is_female = no
						NOR = {
							has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
						}
					}
					AND = {
						is_female = yes
						OR = {
							has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
							has_religion_feature = religion_equal
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_bon
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_bai
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minahasan
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_ryukyuan
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = lustful
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_secret_religion = no
						is_devil_worshiper_trigger = no
						has_character_flag = seclusion_leave_reason_meeting
					}
					has_character_flag = seclusion_leave_reason_walk
					has_character_flag = seclusion_leave_reason_hunt
					has_character_flag = seclusion_leave_reason_duel
				}
			}
		}
	}

# Relative comes clean - brothel
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1963
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1963
	
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_mother
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = yes
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_father
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = yes
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = no
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_son_asking_father
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = no
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		trigger = {
			NAND = {
				intrigue >= 10
				attribute_diff = {
					character = FROM
					attribute = intrigue
					value >= 5
				}
				NOT = {
					trait = trusting
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1963 # Very well!
		
		FROM = {
			opinion = {
				name = let_me_leave
				who = ROOT
			}
			
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1978 } # Permitted to leave
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = paranoid
					AND = {
						is_rival = FROM
						NOT = {
							trait = trusting
						}
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							is_female = no
							NOR = {
								has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
							}
						}
						AND = {
							is_female = yes
							OR = {
								has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
								has_religion_feature = religion_equal
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_bon
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_bai
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minahasan
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_ryukyuan
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = trusting
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			intrigue >= 10
			attribute_diff = {
				character = FROM
				attribute = intrigue
				value >= 5
			}
			NOT = {
				trait = trusting
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1963 # So when exactly are you planning to leave?
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			add_trait_partially_deceitful_effect = yes
		}
		
		FROM = {
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			
			opinion = {
				name = let_me_leave
				who = ROOT
			}
			
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_tailed_by_agents
				months = 1
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1978 } # Relative is being tailed but doesn't know it
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = honest
					NOT = {
						is_rival = FROM
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							is_female = no
							NOR = {
								has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
							}
						}
						AND = {
							is_female = yes
							OR = {
								has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
								has_religion_feature = religion_equal
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_bon
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_bai
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minahasan
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_ryukyuan
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = honest
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1963 # I forbid it!
		
		FROM = {
			opinion = {
				name = forbade_me_to_leave
				who = ROOT
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = trusting
					NOT = {
						is_rival = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = deceitful
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = paranoid
			}
		}
	}
}

# Relative comes clean - taking a walk
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1971
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1971
	
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_mother
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = yes
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_father
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = yes
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = no
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_son_asking_father
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = no
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		trigger = {
			NAND = {
				intrigue >= 10
				attribute_diff = {
					character = FROM
					attribute = intrigue
					value >= 5
				}
				NOT = {
					trait = trusting
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1971 # Very well!
		
		FROM = {
			opinion = {
				name = let_me_leave
				who = ROOT
			}
			
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1978 } # Permitted to leave
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = paranoid
					AND = {
						is_rival = FROM
						NOT = {
							trait = trusting
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = trusting
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_dumb_trigger = no
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_dumb_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			intrigue >= 10
			attribute_diff = {
				character = FROM
				attribute = intrigue
				value >= 5
			}
			NOT = {
				trait = trusting
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1971 # So when exactly are you planning to leave?
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			add_trait_partially_deceitful_effect = yes
		}
		
		FROM = {
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			
			opinion = {
				name = let_me_leave
				who = ROOT
			}
			
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_tailed_by_agents
				months = 1
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1978 } # Relative is being tailed but doesn't know it
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = honest
					NOT = {
						is_rival = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = honest
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_dumb_trigger = no
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_dumb_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1971 # I forbid it!
		
		FROM = {
			opinion = {
				name = forbade_me_to_leave
				who = ROOT
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = trusting
					NOT = {
						is_rival = FROM
					}
				}
			}

				factor = 0.1
				trait = deceitful
			}

				factor = 10
				trait = paranoid

		ai_chance = {

					NOT = {

					}
				}
			}

				factor = 0.1
				trait = deceitful

		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = yes
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = no
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_son_asking_father
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = no
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		trigger = {
			NAND = {
				intrigue >= 10
				attribute_diff = {
					character = FROM
					attribute = intrigue
					value >= 5
				}
				NOT = {
					trait = trusting
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1976 # Very well!
		
		FROM = {
			opinion = {
				name = let_me_leave
				who = ROOT
			}
			
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1978 } # Permitted to leave
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = paranoid
					AND = {
						is_rival = FROM
						NOT = {
							trait = trusting
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = trusting
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = duelist
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				FROM = {
					can_be_commander_no_fromfrom_trigger = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		trigger = {
			intrigue >= 10
			attribute_diff = {
				character = FROM
				attribute = intrigue
				value >= 5
			}
			NOT = {
				trait = trusting
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1976 # So when exactly are you planning to leave?
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			add_trait_partially_deceitful_effect = yes
		}
		
		FROM = {
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			
			opinion = {
				name = let_me_leave
				who = ROOT
			}
			
			reverse_opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_tailed_by_agents
				months = 1
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1978 } # Relative is being tailed but doesn't know it
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = honest
					NOT = {
						is_rival = FROM
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = honest
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = duelist
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				FROM = {
					can_be_commander_no_fromfrom_trigger = no
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {

				name = forbade_me_to_leave
				who = ROOT
			}

# Relative is outside - determine if they are successfully followed as well as what happens
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1979
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		event_target:seclusion_host = {
			in_seclusion = yes
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			
			seclusion_leave_reason_brothel = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.1981 } # Brothel visit
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						event_target:seclusion_host = {
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = opinion_tailed_by_agents
							}
						}
					}
					
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								intrigue >= 10
							}
							
							mult_modifier = {
								factor = 2
								intrigue >= 15
							}
							
							event_target:seclusion_host = {
								character_event = { id = tianxia.1980 } # Ruler's agents lost sight of relative
							}
						}
						
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								intrigue < 20
							}
							
							event_target:seclusion_host = {
								character_event = { id = tianxia.1982 } # Ruler's agents report on brothel visit
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			seclusion_leave_reason_gambling = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.1987 } # Gambling
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						event_target:seclusion_host = {
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = opinion_tailed_by_agents
							}
						}
					}
					
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								intrigue >= 10
							}
							
							mult_modifier = {
								factor = 2
								intrigue >= 15
							}
							
							event_target:seclusion_host = {
								character_event = { id = tianxia.1980 } # Ruler's agents lost sight of relative
							}
						}
						
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								intrigue < 20
							}
							
							event_target:seclusion_host = {
								character_event = { id = tianxia.1988 } # Ruler's agents report on gambling
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			seclusion_leave_reason_drinking = {
				random_friend = {
					limit = {
						is_landed = no
						distance = {
							who = ROOT
							value < 200
						}
						is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no
					}
					
					save_event_target_as = drinking_buddy
				}
					
				character_event = { id = tianxia.1989 } # Drinking with friends
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						event_target:seclusion_host = {
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = opinion_tailed_by_agents
							}
						}
					}
					
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								intrigue >= 10
							}
							
							mult_modifier = {
								factor = 2
								intrigue >= 15
							}
							
							event_target:seclusion_host = {
								character_event = { id = tianxia.1980 } # Ruler's agents lost sight of relative
							}
						}
						
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								intrigue < 20
							}
							
							event_target:seclusion_host = {
								character_event = { id = tianxia.1990 } # Ruler's agents report on drinking
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			seclusion_leave_reason_meeting = {
				clear_flags_with_prefix = seclusion_relative_meeting_
				
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						trigger = {
							NOR = {
								AND = {
									NOT = {
										same_society_as = event_target:seclusion_host
									}
									OR = {
										society_member_of = secret_religious_cult
										is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
									}
								}
								AND = {
									OR = {
										has_plot = plot_kill_character
										has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
									}
									NOT = {
										any_plot_backer = {
											character = event_target:seclusion_host
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
						
						set_character_flag = seclusion_relative_meeting_harmless
					}
					
					25 = {
						trigger = {
							society_member_of = secret_religious_cult
							NOT = {
								same_society_as = event_target:seclusion_host
							}
						}
						
						set_character_flag = seclusion_relative_meeting_srs
					}
					
					25 = {
						trigger = {
							is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
							NOT = {
								same_society_as = event_target:seclusion_host
							}
						}
						
						set_character_flag = seclusion_relative_meeting_dw
					}
					
					25 = {
						trigger = {
							OR = {
								has_plot = plot_kill_character
								has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
							}
							NOT = {
								any_plot_backer = {
									character = event_target:seclusion_host
								}
							}
						}
						
						plot_target_char = {
							save_event_target_as = murder_target
						}
						
						set_character_flag = seclusion_relative_meeting_assassin
					}
				}
				
				trigger_switch = {
					on_trigger = has_character_flag
					
					seclusion_relative_meeting_harmless = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1991 } # Harmless meeting
					}
					
					seclusion_relative_meeting_srs = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1993 } # SRS meeting
					}
					
					seclusion_relative_meeting_dw = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1995 } # DW meeting
					}
					
					seclusion_relative_meeting_assassin = {
						character_event = { id = tianxia.1997 } # Harmless meeting
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						event_target:seclusion_host = {
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = opinion_tailed_by_agents
							}
						}
					}
					
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								intrigue >= 10
							}
							
							mult_modifier = {
								factor = 2
								intrigue >= 15
							}
							
							event_target:seclusion_host = {
								character_event = { id = tianxia.1980 } # Ruler's agents lost sight of relative
							}
						}
						
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								intrigue < 20
							}
							
							trigger_switch = {
								on_trigger = has_character_flag
								
								seclusion_relative_meeting_harmless = {
									event_target:seclusion_host = {
										character_event = { id = tianxia.1992 } # Ruler's agents report on harmless meeting
									}
								}
								
								seclusion_relative_meeting_srs = {
									event_target:seclusion_host = {
										character_event = { id = tianxia.1994 } # Ruler's agents report on SRS meeting
									}
								}
								
								seclusion_relative_meeting_dw = {
									event_target:seclusion_host = {
										character_event = { id = tianxia.1996 } # Ruler's agents report on DW meeting
									}
								}
								
								seclusion_relative_meeting_assassin = {
									event_target:seclusion_host = {
										character_event = { id = tianxia.1998 } # Ruler's agents report on assassin meeting
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			seclusion_leave_reason_walk = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.1999 } # Taking a walk
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						event_target:seclusion_host = {
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = opinion_tailed_by_agents
							}
						}
					}
					
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								intrigue >= 10
							}
							
							mult_modifier = {
								factor = 2
								intrigue >= 15
							}
							
							event_target:seclusion_host = {
								character_event = { id = tianxia.1980 } # Ruler's agents lost sight of relative

								intrigue < 20
							}
							
							event_target:seclusion_host = {
								character_event = { id = tianxia.2000 } # Ruler's agents report on walk
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			seclusion_leave_reason_hunt = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							trait = cannibal
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2001 } # Normal hunt
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2003 } # Cannibal hunt
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						event_target:seclusion_host = {
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = opinion_tailed_by_agents

					}

								who = event_target:seclusion_host
								value < 200

							is_inaccessible_or_incapable_trigger = no

							always = yes

								who = ROOT
								modifier = opinion_tailed_by_agents

						}
					}
					
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								intrigue >= 10
							}
							
							mult_modifier = {
								factor = 2
								intrigue >= 15
							}
							
							event_target:seclusion_host = {
								character_event = { id = tianxia.1980 } # Ruler's agents lost sight of relative
							}
						}
						
						50 = {
							trigger = {
								intrigue < 20
							}

									}
								}

							}
						}

# Did the relative meet someone interesting enough to bring back?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1983
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1983
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				trait = master_seducer
				trait = master_seductress
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1983 # Bringing back a prostitute
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				prefers_men_trigger = yes
			}
			
			create_character = {
				female = no
				age = 18
				dynasty = none
				culture = event_target:seclusion_location
				religion = event_target:seclusion_location
				trait = attractive
				trait = lustful
				trait = kind
				trait = gregarious
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = no
				}
				
				new_character = {
					add_trait = homosexual
				}
			}
			
			random = {
				chance = 10
				new_character = {
					add_trait = lovers_pox
				}
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			create_character = {
				female = yes
				age = 18
				dynasty = none
				culture = event_target:seclusion_location
				religion = event_target:seclusion_location

					add_trait = lovers_pox
				}
			}
		}

			add_lover = ROOT
			
			save_event_target_as = sex_partner
		}
		
		unprotected_sex_effect_event_target = yes

						}

				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.1985 } # Inform host
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = proud
				}
			}
		}
	}

		trigger = {
			OR = {
				trait = master_seducer
				trait = master_seductress
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1983 # Bringing back a minor noble

				prefers_men_trigger = yes
