# Relative comes back with lover
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1984
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCA_TIANXIA_1984
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				can_marry = yes
				NOT = {
					trait = homosexual
				}
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCB_TIANXIA_1984
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				trait = homosexual
			}
		}
	}
	
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_mother
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = yes
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_father
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = yes
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = no
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_son_asking_father
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_female = no
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:seclusion_location = {
					has_disease = yes
				}
			}
			
			set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				can_marry = yes
				NOT = {
					trait = homosexual
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1984 # Fine; get married!
		
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
					dynasty = ROOT
					ROOT = {
						NOR = {
							has_religion_feature = religion_patriarchal
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_batak
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_yi
							AND = {
								OR = {
									religion_group = muslim
									religion = confucian
								}
								has_law = agnatic_succession
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				add_spouse_matrilineal = event_target:sex_partner
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					is_female = no
					dynasty = ROOT
					ROOT = {
						NOR = {
							has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
						}
					}
				}
				
				add_spouse = event_target:sex_partner
			}
			
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						OR = {
							has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
						}
					}
				}
				
				add_spouse_matrilineal = event_target:sex_partner
			}
			
			else = {
				add_spouse = event_target:sex_partner
			}
		}
		
		event_target:sex_partner = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = in_seclusion
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					event_target:sex_partner = {
						is_lowborn = no
					}
					trait = proud
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = humble
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = kind
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_rival = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = ambitious
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				higher_real_tier_than = duke
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				can_marry = yes
				NOT = {
					trait = homosexual
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1984 # You can stay, but you're certainly not marrying!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			remove_friend = FROM
		}
		
		event_target:sex_partner = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = in_seclusion
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = content
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				can_marry = yes
				NOT = {
					trait = homosexual
				}
			}
			NOR = {
				is_friend = FROM
				is_rival = FROM
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_1984 # They're not staying and you're not marrying them!

		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no

			add_rival = ROOT

			opinion = {
				name = expelled_lover_from_seclusion
				who = ROOT
			}
		}
		
		event_target:sex_partner = {
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = content
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				can_marry = yes
				NOT = {
					trait = homosexual
				}
			}
			is_rival = FROM
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_1984 # Get out, both of you!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			add_trait_partially_cruel_effect = yes
		}

		FROM = {
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion

			opinion = {
				name = expelled_me_from_seclusion
				who = ROOT
			}
		}
		
		event_target:sex_partner = {
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
		}
		
		# Lovers marry in secret
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				show_scope_change = no
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_female = yes
						dynasty = ROOT
						ROOT = {
							NOR = {
								has_religion_feature = religion_patriarchal
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_batak
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_yi
								AND = {
									OR = {
										religion_group = muslim
										religion = confucian
									}
									has_law = agnatic_succession
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					add_spouse_matrilineal = event_target:sex_partner
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_female = no
						dynasty = ROOT
						ROOT = {
							NOR = {
								has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
							}
						}
					}
					
					add_spouse = event_target:sex_partner
				}
				
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						ROOT = {
							OR = {
								has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
								has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau
							}
						}
					}
					
					add_spouse_matrilineal = event_target:sex_partner
				}
				
				else = {
					add_spouse = event_target:sex_partner
				}
			}
		}
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1986 } # Inform FROM
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = cruel
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = content
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					can_marry = no
					trait = homosexual
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTE_TIANXIA_1984 # You can stay!
		
		event_target:sex_partner = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = in_seclusion
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					event_target:sex_partner = {
						is_lowborn = no
					}
					trait = proud

			}

			}

				factor = 0.1
				trait = ambitious
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				higher_real_tier_than = duke
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					can_marry = no
					trait = homosexual
				}
			}
			is_rival = FROM

		name = EVTOPTF_TIANXIA_1984 # Get out, both of you!

		}

		FROM = {

				name = expelled_me_from_seclusion
				who = ROOT
			}
		}

			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
		}

		}

			}

				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = content
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					has_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
				}
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
					}
				}
				
				set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2012 days = 30 random = 30 }
				
				event_target:sex_partner = {
					set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2012 days = 30 random = 30 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					is_female = yes

				limit = {
					is_female = no

							has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal
							has_religion_feature = religion_feature_minangkabau

	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1987
	picture = GFX_evt_brothel
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy

	is_triggered_only = yes

		random_list = {
			50 = {
				wealth = 25
			}
			
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					NOT = {
						trait = game_master
					}
				}
				wealth = -25
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = event_target:seclusion_host
						modifier = expelled_me_from_seclusion
					}
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2011 days = 2 } # Return to seclusion
		}
	}
}

				chance = 25

					name = epic_hangover
					years = 5
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:drinking_buddy = {
					always = yes
				}
			}
			
			event_target:drinking_buddy = {
				opinion = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = opinion_fun_time
				}
				
				reverse_opinion = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = opinion_fun_time
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = event_target:seclusion_host
						modifier = expelled_me_from_seclusion
					}
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2011 days = 2 } # Return to seclusion
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = tianxia.1994
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_1994

	immediate = {

			if = {

					}
				}

					chance = 25
					set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	# Largely reuses MNM.70200's setup

	option = { # Execute them!
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				tolerates_other_religions_trigger = no
				event_target:accused_apostate = {
					is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
						has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
					}
				}
			}
		 }
		 
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1994

		piety = 10

		tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				show_scope_change = no
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
						e_china = {
							holder_scope = {
								is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
								OR = {
									character = FROM
									AND = {
										is_close_relative = FROM
										dynasty = FROM
									}
								}
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
					}

						limit = {
							is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						}
						ROOT = {
							detract_grace_super_huge_effect = yes
						}
					}
					else = {
						ROOT = {
							detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
						}
					}
				}

					death_reason = death_execution_burning

				}
			}
		}

		FROM = {
			save_event_target_as = kinslayer_target
		}

		add_kinslayer_trait_effect = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			FROM = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes

							has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
							has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
						}
					}

					add_character_modifier = {
						name = known_satanist
						duration = -1
					}
				}
				else = {
					add_character_modifier = {
						name = accused_apostate
						duration = -1
					}
				}

						religion = ROOT
						controls_religion = no
						religion_has_excommunication = yes
					}

					add_trait = excommunicated
				}
			}

			opinion = {
				name = opinion_accused_apostate
				who = FROM
				years = 100
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_nickname = no

					FROM = {
						NOT = { character = event_target:innocent_victim }
					}
				}

				random_list = {
					500 = { } # No Nickname
					5 = { give_nickname = nick_the_cleansing_flame }
					5 = { give_nickname = nick_the_witch_hunter }
					5 = { give_nickname = nick_the_inquisitor }
					5 = { give_nickname = nick_the_purifier }
				}
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {

			}

			}

						OR = {

								AND = {
									is_close_relative = event_target:accused_apostate
									dynasty = event_target:accused_apostate
								}
							}
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
					}
					ROOT = {
						detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
					}
				}
				else = {
					ROOT = {
						detract_grace_major_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes

					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
						has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
					}
				}

				add_character_modifier = {
					name = known_satanist
					duration = -1
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					name = accused_apostate
					duration = -1
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					religion = ROOT
					controls_religion = no
					religion_has_excommunication = yes
				}

				add_trait = excommunicated
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100

			trigger = { trait = trusting }
		}
	}

	option = { # Let them go
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_1994

		piety = -10

		opinion = {
			name = opinion_apostate_timer
			who = event_target:accused_apostate
			years = 10
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			trigger = { trait = trusting }
		}
	}
}

character_event = {

	picture = GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_1998 # I see...
		
		reveal_plot = FROM
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			random = {
				chance = 25
				add_trait_partially_arbitrary_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:seclusion_location = {
						has_disease = yes
					}
				}
				
				random = {
					chance = 25
					set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				event_target:murder_target = {
					NOR = {
						character = ROOT
						is_friend = ROOT
						is_lover = ROOT
						AND = {
							is_close_relative = ROOT
							NOT = {
								is_rival = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_1998 # Imprison them at once!
		
		FROM = {
			plot_target_char = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
						e_china = {
							holder_scope = {
								is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
								OR = {
									character = PREVPREV
									AND = {
										is_close_relative = PREVPREV
										dynasty = PREVPREV
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						}
						FROM = {
							detract_grace_super_huge_effect = yes
						}
					}
					else = {
						FROM = {
							detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		reveal_plot = FROM
		
		reverse_imprison = FROM
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:seclusion_location = {
						has_disease = yes
					}
				}
				
				random = {
					chance = 25
					set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				OR = {
					event_target:murder_target = {
						NOT = {
							is_rival = ROOT
						}
					}
					is_rival = FROM
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = tianxia.2001
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2001
	picture = GFX_evt_bandits
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2001 # Time to hunt!
		
		pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = event_target:seclusion_host
						modifier = expelled_me_from_seclusion
					}
				}
			}
			
			event_target:seclusion_host = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							modifier = opinion_knows_you_left
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2005 days = 2 } # Inform host
				}
				
				else = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2006 days = 2 } # Inform host
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			change_variable = {
				which = seclusion_food
				value = 3
			}
		}
		
		FROM = {
			add_character_modifier = {
				modifier = in_seclusion
				duration = -1
			}
		}
	}
}

# Relative duels rival good target - how does it go?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2007
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2007
	picture = GFX_evt_duel_scene
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2007 # Time to fight!
		
		# Intentionally not a real duel; it'd be weird with a possible duel death when the host might be unaware
		
		random_list = {
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					combat_rating_diff = {
						who = event_target:duel_opponent
						value >= 25
					}
				}
				
				prestige = 25
				
				event_target:duel_opponent = {
					prestige = -25
					
					add_trait = wounded
				}
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					combat_rating_diff = {
						who = event_target:duel_opponent
						value >= -10
					}
					combat_rating_diff = {
						who = event_target:duel_opponent
						value < 25
					}
				}
				
				prestige = 25
				
				add_trait = wounded
				
				event_target:duel_opponent = {
					prestige = -25
					
					add_trait = wounded
				}
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					combat_rating_diff = {
						who = event_target:duel_opponent
						value <= -25
					}
				}
				
				prestige = -25
					
				add_trait = wounded
				
				event_target:duel_opponent = {
					prestige = 25
				}
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					combat_rating_diff = {
						who = event_target:duel_opponent
						value <= 10
					}
					combat_rating_diff = {
						who = event_target:duel_opponent
						value > -25
					}
				}
				
				prestige = -25
					
				add_trait = wounded
				
				event_target:duel_opponent = {
					prestige = 25
					
					add_trait = wounded
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = event_target:seclusion_host
						modifier = expelled_me_from_seclusion
					}
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2011 days = 2 } # Return to seclusion
		}
	}
}

	id = tianxia.2009
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2009
	picture = GFX_evt_duel_scene
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2009 # Time to fight!
		
		# Intentionally not a real duel; it'd be weird with a possible duel death when the host might be unaware
		
		random_list = {
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					combat_rating >= 25
				}
				
				prestige = 25
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					combat_rating >= 0
					combat_rating < 50
				}
				
				prestige = 25
				
				add_trait = wounded
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					combat_rating < 50
				}
				
				prestige = -25
					
				add_trait = wounded
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = event_target:seclusion_host
						modifier = expelled_me_from_seclusion
					}
				}
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2011 days = 2 } # Return to seclusion
		}
	}
}

character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2011
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2011
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		is_ruler = no
		event_target:seclusion_host = {
			in_seclusion = yes
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2011 # Back home!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:seclusion_location = {
					has_disease = yes
				}
			}
			
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						has_character_flag = seclusion_leave_reason_walk
					}
					
					# No disease
				}
				
				50 = {
					set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2012 days = 30 random = 30 }
				}
			}
			
			host = {
				random = {
					chance = 10
					set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
				}
			}
		}
		
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = in_seclusion
			duration = -1
		}
	}
}

# Someone suspects there might be tunnels - ask liege for maps or go hunting?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2013
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2013
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_fake_maps # In case this fired before
		
		FROM = {
			save_event_target_as = seclusion_host
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_rival = FROM
				AND = {
					trait = paranoid
					NOT = {
						is_friend = FROM
					}
				}
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_plot = plot_kill_character
						has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
					}
					NOT = {
						any_plot_backer = {
							character = FROM
						}
					}
				}
				FROM = {
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2013 # I will not ask!
		
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					intrigue >= 10
					attribute_diff = {
						character = FROM
						attribute = intrigue
						value >= 0
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					intrigue >= 15
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					intrigue >= 20
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2019 days = 5 random = 2 } # Let's see what happens
			}
			
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					FROM = {
						is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					FROM = {
						has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_sneaky
					}
				}
				
				FROM = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2015 } # Liege notices courtier sneaking about
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				is_rival = FROM
				AND = {
					trait = paranoid
					NOT = {
						is_friend = FROM
					}
				}
				AND = {
					OR = {
						has_plot = plot_kill_character
						has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
					}
					NOT = {
						any_plot_backer = {
							character = FROM
						}
					}
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2013 # I might as well ask...
		
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					is_friend = FROM
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					is_lover = FROM
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					is_voter = yes
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					is_close_relative = FROM
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = honest
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = just
				}
				
				FROM = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2014 } # Liege asked for maps
				}
			}
			
			50 = {
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = deceitful
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = arbitrary
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = paranoid
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					trait = master_schemer
				}
				
				# Don't bother to ask
				random_list = {
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							intrigue >= 10
							attribute_diff = {
								character = FROM
								attribute = intrigue
								value >= 0
							}
						}
						
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							intrigue >= 15
						}
						
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							intrigue >= 20
						}
						
						character_event = { id = tianxia.2019 days = 5 random = 2 } # Let's see what happens
					}
					
					50 = {
						trigger = {
							FROM = {
								is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							}
						}
						
						mult_modifier = {
							factor = 2
							FROM = {
								has_character_flag = seclusion_type_is_sneaky
							}
						}
						
						FROM = {
							character_event = { id = tianxia.2015 } # Liege notices courtier sneaking about
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Liege asked for maps - decide what to do
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2014
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2014
	picture = GFX_evt_castle_construction
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2014 # Of course you shall have your maps!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			random = {
				chance = 25
				add_trait_partially_trusting_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		event_target:tunnel_courtier = {
			opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_got_castle_maps
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2017 }
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = event_target:tunnel_courtier
					NOT = {
						trait = trusting
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = trusting
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = paranoid
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			intrigue >= 15
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2014 # I'll give you some maps, just not the real ones...
		
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					trait = honest
				}
				
				remove_trait_honest_effect = yes
			}
			
			else = {
				random = {
					chance = 25
					add_trait_deceitful_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		event_target:tunnel_courtier = {
			opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_got_castle_maps
			}
			
			set_character_flag = seclusion_fake_maps
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2017 }
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = trusting
					NOT = {
						is_rival = event_target:tunnel_courtier
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_friend = event_target:tunnel_courtier
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_lover = event_target:tunnel_courtier
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_close_relative = event_target:tunnel_courtier
				NOT = {
					is_rival = event_target:tunnel_courtier
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = paranoid
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = honest
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2014 # Absolutely not!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			random = {
				chance = 25
				add_trait_partially_paranoid_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		event_target:tunnel_courtier = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2016 }
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					OR = {
						is_friend = event_target:tunnel_courtier
						is_lover = event_target:tunnel_courtier
					}
					trait = trusting
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = trusting
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = deceitful
				intrigue >= 15
			}
		}
	}
}

		}

		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = FROM
					NOT = {
						trait = trusting
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					trait = paranoid
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = trusting
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_friend = FROM
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_lover = FROM
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2015 # I must watch them carefully in the future...
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			random = {
				chance = 25

			who = FROM
			modifier = opinion_suspect_courtier
			months = 12
		}

				NAND = {
					is_rival = FROM
					trait = paranoid
				}

			}

				factor = 10
				is_friend = FROM
			}

				factor = 10
				is_lover = FROM

		}

			}

				chance = 25

			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100

				NOT = {
					trait = trusting
				}
			}

# Did not get maps - drop it or continue?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2018
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2018
	picture = GFX_evt_emissary
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2018 # I can find the tunnels on my own!
		
		character_event = { id = tianxia.2019 days = 5 random = 2 } # Outcome
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = craven
					NOR = {
						trait = stubborn
						trait = proud
						trait = ambitious
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = brave
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2018 # My liege knows best...
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				random = {
					chance = 25
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2064 days = 5 random = 2 } # Reworked vanilla tunnel events
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					OR = {
						trait = stubborn
						trait = proud
						trait = ambitious
					}
					OR = {
						is_rival = FROM
						AND = {
							OR = {
								has_plot = plot_kill_character
								has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
							}
							plot_target_char = {
								character = FROM
							}
						}
					}
					NOT = {
						trait = craven
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = craven
			}
		}
	}

				character_event = { id = tianxia.2043 } # Found other courtier attending DW meeting, and it was a trap
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:seclusion_host = {
						NOT = {
							has_character_flag = food_smuggler_deal
						}
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2044 } # Found food smugglers
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					intrigue >= 10
					event_target:seclusion_host = {
						any_courtier = {
							intrigue < 20
							is_adult = yes
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							prisoner = no
							in_seclusion = yes
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
							OR = {
								has_plot = plot_kill_character
								has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
							}
							NOT = {
								any_plot_backer = {
									character = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					intrigue >= 15
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					intrigue >= 20
				}
				
				event_target:seclusion_host = {
					random_courtier = {
						limit = {
							intrigue < 20
							is_adult = yes
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
							prisoner = no
							in_seclusion = yes
							NOR = {
								is_friend = ROOT
								is_lover = ROOT
							}
							OR = {
								has_plot = plot_kill_character
								has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
							}
							NOT = {
								any_plot_backer = {
									character = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
						
						save_event_target_as = sneaky_courtier
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2051 } # Found someone trying to get an assassin inside
			}
			
			25 = {
				trigger = {
					intrigue >= 10
					OR = {
						is_rival = event_target:seclusion_host
						AND = {
							OR = {
								has_plot = plot_kill_character
								has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
							}
							plot_target_char = {
								character = event_target:seclusion_host
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					intrigue >= 15
				}
				
				mult_modifier = {
					factor = 2
					intrigue >= 20
				}
				
				event_target:seclusion_host = {
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2057 days = 5 random = 10  } # Assassination attempt
				}
			}
			
			25 = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2062 } # Someone might be trying to get in (vanilla event chain)
			}
		}
	}
}

# Liege informed by tunnel courtier of SRS meeting - reveal identities of relevant SRS member to liege
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2040
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2040
	picture = GFX_evt_secret_ceremony
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
			any_society_member = {
				limit = {
					host = {
						character = ROOT
					}
					prisoner = no
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
					OR = {
						in_seclusion = yes
						ROOT = {
							in_seclusion = no
						}
					}
				}
				
				increase_visibility_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	# Largely reuses MNM.70200's setup

	option = { # Execute them!
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				tolerates_other_religions_trigger = no
				event_target:accused_apostate = {
					is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
						has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
					}
				}
			}
		 }
		 
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2040

		piety = 10

		tooltip = {
			event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
				show_scope_change = no
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
						e_china = {
							holder_scope = {
								is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
								OR = {
									character = event_target:sneaky_courtier
									AND = {
										is_close_relative = event_target:sneaky_courtier
										dynasty = event_target:sneaky_courtier
									}
								}
								NOT = {
									character = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						}
						ROOT = {
							detract_grace_super_huge_effect = yes
						}
					}
					else = {
						ROOT = {
							detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
						}
					}
				}

				death = {
					death_reason = death_execution_burning
					killer = ROOT
				}
			}
		}

		event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
			save_event_target_as = kinslayer_target
		}

		add_kinslayer_trait_effect = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes

						OR = {
							has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
							has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
						}
					}

					add_character_modifier = {
						name = known_satanist
						duration = -1
					}
				}
				else = {
					add_character_modifier = {
						name = accused_apostate
						duration = -1
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						religion = ROOT
						controls_religion = no
						religion_has_excommunication = yes
					}

					add_trait = excommunicated
				}
			}

			opinion = {
				name = opinion_accused_apostate
				who = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				years = 100
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_nickname = no

					event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
						NOT = { character = event_target:innocent_victim }
					}
				}

				random_list = {
					500 = { } # No Nickname
					5 = { give_nickname = nick_the_cleansing_flame }
					5 = { give_nickname = nick_the_witch_hunter }
					5 = { give_nickname = nick_the_inquisitor }
					5 = { give_nickname = nick_the_purifier }
				}
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = lunatic
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = possessed
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = cruel
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = paranoid
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				trait = arbitrary
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 100
				trait = zealous
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				tolerates_other_religions_trigger = yes
				event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
					NAND = {
						is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes

						OR = {
							has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
							has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Imprison them
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2040

		reverse_imprison = event_target:sneaky_courtier

		opinion = {
			name = opinion_accused_apostate
			who = event_target:sneaky_courtier
			years = 100
		}

		event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
			show_scope_change = no
				
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
					e_china = {
						holder_scope = {
							is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
							OR = {
								character = event_target:sneaky_courtier
								AND = {
									is_close_relative = event_target:sneaky_courtier
									dynasty = event_target:sneaky_courtier
								}
							}
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
					}
					ROOT = {
						detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
					}
				}
				else = {
					ROOT = {
						detract_grace_major_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes

					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = under_suspicion

				}

				}

				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = honest
					NOT = {
						is_rival = event_target:seclusion_host
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = greedy
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = deceitful
			}
		}
	}

		}

# Discovered someone attempting to sneak an assassin in
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2051
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2051
	picture = GFX_evt_hiding_from_pursuers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2051 # I will blackmail them!
		
		add_trait_partially_greedy_effect = yes
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						NOR = {
							is_rival = event_target:sneaky_courtier
							event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
								OR = {
									has_plot = plot_kill_character
									has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
								}
								plot_target_char = {
									character = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2052 } # Blackmail successful
				}
				
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						intrigue < 20
						OR = {
							is_rival = event_target:sneaky_courtier
							event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
								OR = {
									has_plot = plot_kill_character
									has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
								}
								plot_target_char = {
									character = ROOT
								}
							}
							event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
								OR = {
									trait = deceitful
									trait = arbitrary
									trait = wroth
									trait = cruel
								}
								NOR = {
									trait = kind
									trait = honest
									trait = patient
									trait = just
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						intrigue < 5
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2053 } # Killed by assassin
				}
				
				50 = {
					trigger = {
						OR = {
							is_rival = event_target:sneaky_courtier
							event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
								OR = {
									has_plot = plot_kill_character
									has_plot = plot_kill_spouse
								}
								plot_target_char = {
									character = ROOT
								}
							}
							event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
								OR = {
									trait = deceitful
									trait = arbitrary
									trait = wroth
									trait = cruel
								}
								NOR = {
									trait = kind
									trait = honest
									trait = patient
									trait = just
								}
							}
						}
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						intrigue >= 10
					}
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 2
						intrigue >= 15
					}
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2054 } # Attacked by assassin, got away
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = craven
					trait = just
					trait = honest
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = brave
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2051 # I will inform my liege!
		
		event_target:seclusion_host = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2056 } # Inform liege
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_friend = event_target:seclusion_host
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_lover = event_target:seclusion_host
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_rival = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				NOT = {
					trait = trusting
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Successfully blackmailed courtier
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2052
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2052
	picture = GFX_evt_bribe_accepted
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2052 # Excellent!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Conclave"
				owes_favor_to = event_target:sneaky_courtier
			}
			
			reverse_remove_favor = event_target:sneaky_courtier
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Conclave"
				NOT = {
					holds_favor_on = event_target:sneaky_courtier
				}
			}
			
			add_favor = event_target:sneaky_courtier
		}
		
		else = {
			wealth = 50
			event_target:sneaky_courtier = {
				wealth = -50
			}
		}
	}
}

# Captured sneaker, what do you want to do?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2067
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2067
	picture = GFX_evt_hiding_from_pursuers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	prisoner = no
	
	trigger = {
		is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
		in_seclusion = yes
	}
	
	immediate = {
		capital_scope = {
			save_event_target_as = seclusion_location
		}
		
		create_character = {
			age = 25
			female = 50
			dynasty = none
			culture = event_target:seclusion_location
			religion = event_target:seclusion_location
			random_traits = no
			trait = lustful # Claims to be inside for a lover regardless
			trait = groomed # Claims to be inside for a lover regardless
			trait = tough_soldier
			health = 7
			attributes = {
				intrigue = 9
				diplomacy = 5
				stewardship = 4
			}
		}
		
		new_character = {
			random = {
				chance = 10
				add_trait = homosexual
			}
			
			# They're described as talking to themself
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					add_trait = lunatic
				}
				50 = {
					add_trait = possessed
				}
			}
			
			# Archetype
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_silent }
					}
					
					add_trait = shy
					add_trait = patient
					
					give_nickname = nick_the_silent
				}
				
				10 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_hollow }
					}
					
					add_trait = depressed
					add_trait = envious
					
					give_nickname = nick_the_hollow
				}
				
				10 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_recluse }
					}
					
					add_trait = shy
					add_trait = erudite
					add_trait = cynical
					
					give_nickname = nick_the_recluse
				}
				
				10 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_bard }
					}
					
					add_trait = poet
					add_trait = gregarious
					
					give_nickname = nick_the_bard
				}
				
				10 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_troubadour }
					}
					
					add_trait = poet
					add_trait = gregarious
					
					give_nickname = nick_the_troubadour
				}
				
				10 = {
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_fox }
					}
					
					add_trait = shrewd
					add_trait = deceitful
					add_trait = cruel
					
					give_nickname = nick_the_fox
				}
				
				10 = {
					trigger = { is_female = no }
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_handsome }
					}
					
					remove_trait = groomed
					add_trait = attractive
					add_trait = proud
					
					give_nickname = nick_the_handsome
				}
				
				10 = {
					trigger = { is_female = yes }
					
					mult_modifier = {
						factor = 0.1
						any_courtier = { has_nickname = nick_the_fair }
					}
					
					remove_trait = groomed
					add_trait = attractive
					add_trait = proud
					
					give_nickname = nick_the_fair
				}
			}
			
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					# Nothing special

					set_character_flag = sneaker_is_seducer
				}
				25 = {
					set_character_flag = sneaker_is_revolutionary
				}
				25 = {
					set_character_flag = sneaker_is_assassin
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = captured_sneaker
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2067 # Let's hear them out!
		
		character_event = { id = tianxia.2068 days = 2 } # Heard them out
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOT = {
					trait = paranoid
				}
			}

				factor = 10
				trait = kind
			}
		}
	}

		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2067 # Imprison them!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:captured_sneaker = {
					has_character_flag = sneaker_is_revolutionary
				}
			}

		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {

					trait = trusting
				}
			}

				factor = 10
				trait = kind
			}
		}
	}

			}

				}

	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

				trait = paranoid
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2068 # How sad!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:seclusion_location = {
					has_disease = yes
				}
			}
			
			set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = kind
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				trait = trusting
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2068 # I don't trust you; go to jail!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:seclusion_location = {
					has_disease = yes
				}
			}
			
			set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				trait = cruel
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:captured_sneaker = {
					prisoner = no
				}
			}
			
			event_target:captured_sneaker = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2069 days = 5 random = 2 } # Determine what happens
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = sneaker_is_revolutionary
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2072 days = 5 random = 2 } # Revolutionary's friends cannot get in
		}
	}
}

# Hidden event to determine outcome
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2069
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		host = {
			character = FROM
			in_seclusion = yes
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = sneaker_is_seducer
				FROM = {
					is_adult = yes
					root_attraction_trigger = yes
					is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				}
			}
			
			FROM = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2071 } # Attempted seduction
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = sneaker_is_revolutionary
			}
			
			FROM = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2073 } # Revolutionary lets friends inside
			}
		}
		
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = sneaker_is_assassin
			}
			
			FROM = {
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2078 } # Attempted assassination
			}
		}
		
		else = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2070 } # Sneaker seems to be fitting in just fine
		}
	}
}

# Sneaker at court, attempts seduction of liege
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2071

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCA_TIANXIA_2071
		trigger = {
			event_target:captured_sneaker = {
				is_female = yes
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCB_TIANXIA_2071
		trigger = {
			event_target:captured_sneaker = {
				is_female = no
			}
		}
	}
	
	picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				root_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2071 # I like the sound of that!
		
		remove_trait_chaste_effect = yes
		
		unprotected_sex_effect = yes
		
		piety = -10
		
		ai_chance = {
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = chaste
					trait = celibate
					AND = {
						is_ascetic_trigger = yes
						NOT = {
							trait = lustful
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			factor = 100
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				FROM = {
					is_unattractive_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 5
				FROM = {
					is_attractive_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				root_attraction_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2071 # I don't think so!
		
		piety = 10
		
		random = {
			chance = 25
			remove_trait_lustful_effect = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = lustful
					trait = master_seducer
					trait = master_seductress
				}
			}
			
			factor = 100
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				root_attraction_trigger = no
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2071 # I don't like you that way, but perhaps we could be friends?
		
		reverse_opinion = {
			name = opinion_budding_friendship
			who = event_target:captured_sneaker
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				root_attraction_trigger = no
			}
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_2071 # Leave. Now!
	}
}

# Killed in the fighting
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2074
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2074
	picture = GFX_evt_bloody_man
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2074 # Don't you know who I am!
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:captured_sneaker
			modifier = opinion_regicide_traitor
		}
		
		event_target:captured_sneaker = {
			leave_court_effect = yes
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
					e_china = {
						holder_scope = {
							is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
							OR = {
								character = ROOT
								AND = {
									is_close_relative = ROOT
									dynasty = ROOT
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						ROOT = {
							is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					detract_grace_super_huge_effect = yes
				}
				else = {
					detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
				}
			}
			
			pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		}
		
		death = {
			death_reason = death_execution_torn_apart_by_a_mob
			killer = event_target:captured_sneaker
		}
	}
}

# Courtier isn't a fan of seclusion demands that you open the gates, possibly resulting in a murder attempt if you do not do so
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2096
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2096
	
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_mother
		trigger = {
			event_target:courtier_asking = {
				is_female = yes
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_father
		trigger = {
			event_target:courtier_asking = {
				is_female = yes
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
		trigger = {
			event_target:courtier_asking = {
				is_female = no
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_son_asking_father
		trigger = {
			event_target:courtier_asking = {
				is_female = no
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	portrait = event_target:courtier_asking
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				in_seclusion = yes
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				OR = {
					is_rival = ROOT
					trait = ambitious
					trait = deceitful
					trait = cruel
					trait = arbitrary
					AND = {
						trait = gluttonous
						ROOT = {
							check_variable = {
								which = seclusion_food
								value < 5
							}
						}
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_bad_seclusion_type
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = forbade_me_to_leave
					}
				}
				is_heir = no
				NOT = {
					character = event_target:courtier_asking
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = fallback_courtier
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2096 # Very well...
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = open_the_gates_info_tooltip
			clean_up_seclusion_effect = yes
		}

		remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion

		add_character_modifier = {
			name = recently_opened_gates
			years = 1
		}

		character_event = {
			id = RIP.11557 # Clears starvation modifiers
			days = 40
			random = 20
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			any_courtier = {
				clean_up_seclusion_effect_courtier = yes

				character_event = {
					id = RIP.11557 # Clears starvation modifiers
					days = 40
					random = 20
				}
			}

			any_vassal = {
				limit = {
					is_voter = yes
					is_landed = yes
					vassal_of = ROOT
					in_seclusion = no
					host = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				}

				force_host = ROOT
			}
		}

		if = { # Rejoin your liege's court if both of you are not in seclusion
			limit = {
				is_voter = yes
				liege = { in_seclusion = no }
			}

			force_host = liege

			if = { # Notify employer that councillor is coming out of seclusion
				limit = {
					is_councillor = yes
					liege = { ai = no }
				}

				liege = {
					character_event = {
						id = RIP.12100
						days = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0.9

			trigger = {
				OR = {
					capital_scope = { has_disease = no }
					has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
					is_dumb_trigger = yes
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						trait = craven
					}
					capital_scope = {
						NOT = {
							any_neighbor_province = {
								has_disease = yes
							}
						}
					}
					has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
					is_dumb_trigger = yes
				}
					
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				trait = paranoid
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				trait = craven
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 3
				trait = brave
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 3
				capital_scope = { has_disease = no }
				has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
				trait = gluttonous
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				capital_scope = { has_disease = yes }
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_rival = event_target:courtier_asking
				NOT = {
					trait = trusting
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2096 # You must be joking!
		
		event_target:courtier_asking = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2097 days = 5 random = 10 } # Possible murder attempt
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
		}
	}
}

# Murdered by heir - heir was not discovered
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2099
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2099
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	hide_from = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2099 # No! Not like this!
		
		death = {
			death_reason = death_murder_unknown
			killer = FROM
		}
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				set_character_flag = seclusion_inherit_open_gates # For the AI
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2103 days = 2 } # Open the gates?
			}
		}
	}
}

# Murdered by heir - heir was discoverd

	id = tianxia.2100
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2100
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2100 # This is wrong!
		
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = 10 } # Consequences of discovery
			clear_event_target = target_victim
		
			pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		}
		
		death = {
			death_reason = death_murder
			killer = FROM
		}
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				set_character_flag = seclusion_inherit_open_gates # For the AI
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2103 days = 2 } # Open the gates?
			}
		}
	}
}

# Heir's murder attempt fails - discovered
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2102
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2102
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2102 # What do you think you are doing?
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			add_trait_partially_arbitrary_effect = yes
		}
		
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = 11 } # Consequences of discovery
			clear_event_target = target_victim
		
			pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			random = {
				chance = 10
				add_trait_partially_paranoid_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				trait = trusting
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2102 # Go to jail! Go directly to jail! Do not pass "Go"! Do not collect $200!
		
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = 11 } # Consequences of discovery
			clear_event_target = target_victim
		
			pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		}
		
		FROM = {
			imprison = ROOT
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			random = {
				chance = 10
				add_trait_partially_paranoid_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
	}
}

# Courtier that isn't a fan of seclusion demands that you open the gates, possibly resulting in a murder attempt if you do not do so
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2104
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2104
	
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_mother
		trigger = {
			event_target:courtier_asking = {
				is_female = yes
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_daughter_asking_father
		trigger = {
			event_target:courtier_asking = {
				is_female = yes
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
		trigger = {
			event_target:courtier_asking = {
				is_female = no
				is_opposite_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	picture = {
		picture = GFX_evt_son_asking_father
		trigger = {
			event_target:courtier_asking = {
				is_female = no
				same_sex = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	
	portrait = event_target:courtier_asking
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				in_seclusion = yes
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				OR = {
					is_rival = ROOT
					trait = ambitious
					trait = deceitful
					trait = cruel
					trait = arbitrary
					AND = {
						trait = gluttonous
						ROOT = {
							check_variable = {
								which = seclusion_food
								value < 5
							}
						}
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_bad_seclusion_type
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = forbade_me_to_leave
					}
				}
				is_heir = no
				NOT = {
					character = event_target:courtier_asking
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = fallback_courtier
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2104 # Very well...
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = open_the_gates_info_tooltip
			clean_up_seclusion_effect = yes
		}

		remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion

		add_character_modifier = {
			name = recently_opened_gates
			years = 1
		}

		character_event = {
			id = RIP.11557 # Clears starvation modifiers
			days = 40
			random = 20
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			any_courtier = {
				clean_up_seclusion_effect_courtier = yes

				character_event = {
					id = RIP.11557 # Clears starvation modifiers
					days = 40
					random = 20
				}
			}

			any_vassal = {
				limit = {
					is_voter = yes
					is_landed = yes
					vassal_of = ROOT
					in_seclusion = no
					host = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				}

				force_host = ROOT
			}
		}

		if = { # Rejoin your liege's court if both of you are not in seclusion
			limit = {
				is_voter = yes
				liege = { in_seclusion = no }
			}

			force_host = liege

			if = { # Notify employer that councillor is coming out of seclusion
				limit = {
					is_councillor = yes
					liege = { ai = no }
				}

				liege = {
					character_event = {
						id = RIP.12100
						days = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0.9

			trigger = {
				OR = {
					capital_scope = { has_disease = no }
					has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
					is_dumb_trigger = yes
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						trait = craven
					}
					capital_scope = {
						NOT = {
							any_neighbor_province = {
								has_disease = yes
							}
						}
					}
					has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
					is_dumb_trigger = yes
				}
					
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				trait = paranoid
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				trait = craven
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 3
				trait = brave
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 3
				capital_scope = { has_disease = no }
				has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
				trait = gluttonous
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				capital_scope = { has_disease = yes }
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_rival = event_target:courtier_asking
				NOT = {
					trait = trusting
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2104 # You must be joking!
		
		event_target:courtier_asking = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2105 days = 5 random = 10 } # Possible murder attempt
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
		}
	}
}

# Murdered by courtier - courtier was not discovered
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2107
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2107
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	hide_from = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		player_heir = {
			save_event_target_as = my_heir
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2107 # No! Not like this!
		
		death = {
			death_reason = death_murder_unknown
			killer = FROM
		}
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:my_heir = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							is_adult = no
							prisoner = yes
							is_actually_incapable_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					
					set_character_flag = end_seclusion
					
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2111 days = 2 } # Heir MUST open the gates
				}
				
				else = {
					FROM = {
						save_event_target_as = the_killer
					}
					character_event = { id = tianxia.2112 days = 2 } # Heir might be approached about opening the gates
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			clear_event_target = target_victim
		
			pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		}

		}

						}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2110 # What do you think you are doing?

			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			add_trait_partially_arbitrary_effect = yes
		}
		
		save_event_target_as = target_victim
		
		FROM = {
			character_event = { id = 11 } # Consequences of discovery
			clear_event_target = target_victim
		
			pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes
		}

			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}

			}

				trait = trusting

		}

		}

			imprison = ROOT
		}

			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			random = {
				chance = 10
				add_trait_partially_paranoid_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
	}

# Decide who approaches the heir about the murder attempt
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2112
	hide_window = yes
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		event_target:the_killer = {
			save_event_target_as = courtier_asking
		}
		
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				in_seclusion = yes
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				OR = {
					is_rival = ROOT
					trait = ambitious
					trait = deceitful
					trait = cruel
					trait = arbitrary
					AND = {
						trait = gluttonous
						ROOT = {
							check_variable = {
								which = seclusion_food
								value < 5
							}
						}
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_bad_seclusion_type
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = forbade_me_to_leave
					}
				}
				is_heir = no
				NOT = {
					character = event_target:courtier_asking
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = fallback_courtier
		}
		
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:courtier_asking = {
						is_alive = yes
						prisoner = no
						is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
						in_seclusion = yes
						NOT = {
							character = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2113 } # Heir is asked to open the gates
			}
			
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:fallback_courtier = {
						always = yes
					}
				}
				
				event_target:fallback_courtier = {
					save_event_target_as = courtier_asking
				}
				
				character_event = { id = tianxia.2113 } # Heir is asked to open the gates
			}
				
			50 = {
				trigger = {
					event_target:fallback_courtier = {
						always = no
					}
				}
				
				# Nothing
			}
		}
	}
}

					trait = deceitful
					trait = cruel
					trait = arbitrary
					AND = {
						trait = gluttonous
						ROOT = {
							check_variable = {
								which = seclusion_food
								value < 5
							}
						}
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = opinion_bad_seclusion_type
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = forbade_me_to_leave
					}
				}
				is_heir = no
				NOT = {
					character = event_target:courtier_asking
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = fallback_courtier
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2113 # Very well...
		
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = open_the_gates_info_tooltip
			clean_up_seclusion_effect = yes
		}

		remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion

		add_character_modifier = {
			name = recently_opened_gates
			years = 1
		}

		character_event = {
			id = RIP.11557 # Clears starvation modifiers
			days = 40
			random = 20
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			any_courtier = {
				clean_up_seclusion_effect_courtier = yes

				character_event = {
					id = RIP.11557 # Clears starvation modifiers
					days = 40
					random = 20
				}
			}

			any_vassal = {
				limit = {
					is_voter = yes
					is_landed = yes
					vassal_of = ROOT
					in_seclusion = no
					host = { NOT = { character = ROOT } }
				}

				force_host = ROOT
			}
		}

		if = { # Rejoin your liege's court if both of you are not in seclusion
			limit = {
				is_voter = yes
				liege = { in_seclusion = no }
			}

			force_host = liege

			if = { # Notify employer that councillor is coming out of seclusion
				limit = {
					is_councillor = yes
					liege = { ai = no }
				}

				liege = {
					character_event = {
						id = RIP.12100
						days = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0.9

			trigger = {
				OR = {
					capital_scope = { has_disease = no }
					has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
					is_dumb_trigger = yes
				}
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						trait = craven
					}
					capital_scope = {
						NOT = {
							any_neighbor_province = {
								has_disease = yes
							}
						}
					}
					has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
					is_dumb_trigger = yes
				}
					
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				trait = paranoid
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				trait = craven
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 3
				trait = brave
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 3
				capital_scope = { has_disease = no }
				has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 3
				has_character_modifier = starving_food_shortage
				trait = gluttonous
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				capital_scope = { has_disease = yes }
			}

			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				is_rival = event_target:courtier_asking
				NOT = {
					trait = trusting
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2113 # You must be joking!
		
		event_target:courtier_asking = {
			character_event = { id = tianxia.2105 days = 5 random = 10 } # Possible murder attempt
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
		}
	}
}

# Low on food - expell "useless" courtier?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2119
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2119
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				in_seclusion = yes
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				is_unimportant_trigger = yes
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						is_close_relative = ROOT
					}
					useless_character_trigger = yes
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				NOR = {
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
					AND = {
						is_close_relative = ROOT
						NOT = {
							is_rival = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = useless_mouth_1
		}
		
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				in_seclusion = yes
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				is_unimportant_trigger = yes
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						is_close_relative = ROOT
					}
					useless_character_trigger = yes
				}
				NOT = {
					character = event_target:useless_mouth_1
				}
			}

				NOR = {
					is_friend = ROOT

							is_rival = ROOT
						}
					}
				}

				is_adult = yes

				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				is_unimportant_trigger = yes

					NOT = {
						is_close_relative = ROOT
					}
					useless_character_trigger = yes
				}
				NOR = {

				text = useless_mouth_expelled

					name = expelled_me_from_seclusion
					who = ROOT
					years = 2
				}

			}

					has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"

							is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
							is_close_relative = PREVPREV
							dynasty = PREVPREV

					}

			}
		}

# Someone caught stealing food - not if there is a food guard
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2124
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2124
	picture = GFX_evt_catching_heretic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2124 # How could you do this?
		
		opinion = {
			name = opinion_food_thief
			who = event_target:food_thief
			years = 5
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_rival = event_target:food_thief
					trait = just
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2124 # Guards! Throw them in a cell!

			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
				e_china = {

						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						character = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			event_target:food_thief = {
				detract_grace_medium_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:food_thief
			modifier = opinion_food_thief
			months = 60
		}
		
		event_target:food_thief = {
			imprison = ROOT
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					is_friend = event_target:food_thief
					is_lover = event_target:food_thief
					AND = {
						is_close_relative = event_target:food_thief
						NOT = {
							is_rival = event_target:food_thief
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_rival = FROM
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTC_TIANXIA_2124 # Guards! Throw them out!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
				e_china = {
					holder_scope = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
						character = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			event_target:food_thief = {
				detract_grace_medium_effect = yes
			}
		}
		
		remove_trait_kind_effect = yes
		
		opinion = {
			who = event_target:food_thief
			modifier = opinion_food_thief
			months = 60
		}
		
		event_target:food_thief = {
			opinion = {
				name = expelled_me_from_seclusion
				who = ROOT
				years = 2
			}
			
			leave_court_effect = yes
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
					e_china = {
						holder_scope = {
							is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
							is_close_relative = PREVPREV
							dynasty = PREVPREV
							NOT = {
								character = PREVPREV
							}
						}
					}
				}
				ROOT = {
					detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					trait = cannibal
					trait = gluttonous
					is_rival = event_target:food_thief
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier= {
				factor = 0.1
				OR = {
					is_friend = event_target:food_thief
					is_lover = event_target:food_thief
					AND = {
						is_close_relative = event_target:food_thief
						NOT = {
							is_rival = event_target:food_thief
						}
					}
				}
				is_rival = event_target:food_thief
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		trigger = {
			is_adult = yes
		}
		
		name = EVTOPTD_TIANXIA_2124 # Looks like meat is back on the menu!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
				e_china = {
					holder_scope = {

						character = ROOT
					}

			}

			}

			factor = 100

					is_lover = event_target:food_thief
					AND = {
						is_close_relative = event_target:food_thief
						NOT = {
							is_rival = event_target:food_thief
						}
					}
					AND = {
						trait = temperate
						NOT = {
							trait = cannibal
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10

			}
