# Low on food - eat people?
character_event = {
	id = tianxia.2125
	desc = EVTDESC_TIANXIA_2125
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				in_seclusion = yes
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				NOR = {
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				is_rival = ROOT
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = to_be_eaten
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2125 # Fresh meat!
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
			}
			add_trait_partially_cruel_effect = yes
		}
		
		clr_character_flag = seclusion_cannibal_supplies
		set_character_flag = seclusion_cannibal_supplies
		
		event_target:to_be_eaten = { # TIANXIATODO: Scripted effect
			save_event_target_as = being_eaten
			
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Jade Dragon"
					e_china = {
						holder_scope = {
							is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
							OR = {
								character = event_target:being_eaten
								AND = {
									is_close_relative = event_target:being_eaten
									dynasty = event_target:being_eaten
								}
							}
							NOT = {
								character = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_chinese_emperor_trigger = yes
					}
					ROOT = {
						detract_grace_super_huge_effect = yes
					}
				}
				else = {
					ROOT = {
						detract_grace_massive_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}
			
			hidden_effect = {
				any_dynasty_member = {
					limit = {
						is_offmap_ruler = no
						is_close_relative = event_target:being_eaten
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
					}

					character_event = {
						id = RIP.11511
						days = 1
					}
				}

				ROOT = {
					add_blood_splatter_effect = yes

					random_list = {
						50 = { # Nothing happens
							trigger = {
								event_target:being_eaten = {
									immortal = no

									trigger_if = {
										limit = { controls_religion = yes }
										religion_group = ROOT
									}
								}
							}
						}

						40 = { # Get sick
							trigger = {
								immortal = no
								NOT = { has_character_flag = demon_child }

								event_target:being_eaten = {
									immortal = no

									trigger_if = {
										limit = { controls_religion = yes }
										religion_group = ROOT
									}
								}
							}

							random_list = {
								50 = { # Food Poisoning
									if = {
										limit = {
											num_of_symptoms < 1
											is_ill = no

											NOR = {
												has_character_flag = developing_illness
												has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
											}
										}

										set_character_flag = got_food_poisoning
										set_character_flag = developing_illness

										character_event = {
											id = RIP.5023
											days = 5
										}
									}
								}

								25 = { # Diarrhea - Not Serious
									if = {
										limit = {
											num_of_symptoms < 1
											is_ill = no

											NOR = {
												has_character_flag = developing_illness
												has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
											}
										}

										set_character_flag = disease_not_serious

										character_event = {
											id = RIP.5002
											days = 5
										}

										character_event = {
											id = RIP.11004
											days = 10
											random = 50
										}
									}
								}

								25 = { # Vomiting - Not Serious
									if = {
										limit = {
											num_of_symptoms < 1
											is_ill = no

											NOR = {
												has_character_flag = developing_illness
												has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
											}
										}

										set_character_flag = disease_not_serious

										character_event = {
											id = RIP.5003
											days = 5
										}

										character_event = {
											id = RIP.11005
											days = 10
											random = 50
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}

						10 = { # Gain power!
							trigger = {
								has_game_rule = {
									name = supernatural_events
									value = on
								}
							}

							event_target:being_eaten = {
								if = {
									limit = {
										controls_religion = yes
										NOT = { religion_group = ROOT }
									}

									trigger_switch = {
										on_trigger = religion_group

										christian         = { ROOT = { set_character_flag = pope_eater } }
										muslim            = { ROOT = { set_character_flag = caliph_eater } }
										pagan_group       = { ROOT = { set_character_flag = fylkir_eater } }
										zoroastrian_group = { ROOT = { set_character_flag = fire_eater } }
										jewish_group      = { ROOT = { set_character_flag = non_kosher } }
										indian_group      = { ROOT = { set_character_flag = dont_even_exist } } # Well, the Tenno does...
									}
								}
							}

							character_event = { id = RIP.11510 }
						}
					}

					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								can_apply_average_nickname_trigger = yes
								AND = {
									can_apply_cool_nickname_trigger = yes
									has_chinese_or_historical_culture_trigger = no
									NOT = {
										culture_group = japonic
									}
								}
							}
						}

						random_list = {
							20 = {
								trigger = {
									can_apply_average_nickname_trigger = yes
								}
								give_nickname = nick_the_cannibal
							}
							5 = {
								trigger = {
									is_female = no
									has_chinese_or_historical_culture_trigger = no
									NOT = {
										culture_group = japonic
									}
								}
								give_nickname = nick_the_dragon
							}
							5 = {
								trigger = {
									is_female = yes
									has_chinese_or_historical_culture_trigger = no
									NOT = {
										culture_group = japonic
									}
								}
								give_nickname = nick_the_dragoness
							}
							15 = {
								trigger = {
									can_apply_average_nickname_trigger = yes
								}
								give_nickname = nick_the_abomination
							}
							5 = {
								trigger = {
									can_apply_average_nickname_trigger = yes
								}
								give_nickname = nick_the_monster
							}
							1000 = {
								mult_modifier = {
									factor = 10
									has_nickname = yes
								}
								# No nickname
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}

			save_event_target_as = kinslayer_target

			ROOT = {
				add_kinslayer_trait_effect = yes
				
				pacifists_lose_piety_effect = yes

				if = {
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							trait = lunatic
							graphical_culture = beargfx
							graphical_culture = dragongfx
						}
					}

					add_trait = lunatic
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							trait = cannibal
							#has_character_modifier = crazy_cannibal
							graphical_culture = beargfx
							graphical_culture = dragongfx
						}
					}

					#add_character_modifier = {
					#	name = crazy_cannibal
					#	duration = -1
					#}
					
					add_trait_cannibal_effect = yes
				}
			}
			
			death = {
				death_reason = death_execution_eaten
				killer = ROOT
			}
		}
		
		hidden_tooltip = {
			change_variable = {
				which = seclusion_food
				value = 1
			}
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NOR = {
					trait = kind
					trait = just
					trait = temperate
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 10
				is_rival = event_target:to_be_eaten
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				NOT = {
					trait = cannibal
				}
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTB_TIANXIA_2125 # No, it would be wrong...
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = event_target:food_thief
					trait = cannibal
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = gluttonous
			}
		}
	}
	
	after = {
		update_seclusion_food_modifier_effect = yes
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	only_playable = yes
	
	trigger = {
		in_seclusion = yes
		any_courtier = {
			is_adult = yes
			is_landed = no
			prisoner = no
			is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
			is_maimed_trigger = no
		}
		any_demesne_province = {
			NOT = {
				has_province_modifier = recent_food_gathering
			}
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = recent_food_gathering
				}
			}

				has_disease = no
			}

		}

				is_adult = yes
				is_landed = no
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				is_maimed_trigger = no
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				# Prefer someone interesting
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
				}
				# With interesting traits
				OR = {
					trait = gardener

					trait = gluttonous

					trait = cruel

					trait = arbitrary
					trait = just
					trait = craven
					AND = {
						trait = zealous
						NOT = {
							religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				# Prefer someone interesting or someone with interesting traits
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
					trait = gardener
					trait = hunter
					trait = gregarious
					trait = temperate
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = kind
					trait = cruel
					trait = proud
					trait = humble
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = just
					trait = craven
					AND = {
						trait = zealous
						NOT = {
							religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = gather_food_option_1
		}
		
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_landed = no
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				is_maimed_trigger = no
				NOT = {
					character = event_target:gather_food_option_1
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				# Prefer someone interesting
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
				}
				# With interesting traits
				OR = {
					trait = gardener
					trait = hunter
					trait = gregarious
					trait = temperate
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = kind
					trait = cruel
					trait = proud
					trait = humble
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = just
					trait = craven
					AND = {
						trait = zealous
						NOT = {
							religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				# Prefer someone interesting or someone with interesting traits
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
					trait = gardener

					trait = gregarious

					trait = kind

					AND = {
						trait = zealous
						NOT = {
							religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			save_event_target_as = gather_food_option_2
		}
		
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_adult = yes
				is_landed = no
				prisoner = no
				is_actually_incapable_trigger = no
				is_maimed_trigger = no

				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				# Prefer someone interesting
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT
				}
				# With interesting traits

					AND = {

						NOT = {
							religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
			preferred_limit = {
				# Prefer someone interesting or someone with interesting traits
				OR = {
					dynasty = ROOT
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					is_friend = ROOT
					is_lover = ROOT

		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_TIANXIA_2126 # Let's send the first courtier!
		
		event_target:gather_food_option_1 = {
			show_scope_change = no
			
			save_event_target_as = gathering_food
			
			remove_character_modifier = in_seclusion
			
			opinion = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = opinion_gathering_food_for_you
				months = 1
			}
			
			character_event = { id = tianxia.1803 } # Decide on approach
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
			
			trigger = {
				NAND = {
					is_rival = event_target:gather_food_option_1
					NOT = {
						trait = trusting
					}
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				true_religion = event_target:seclusion_food_province
				NOT = {
					religion = event_target:gather_food_option_1
				}
				event_target:gather_food_option_1 = {
					trait = zealous
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 2
				opinion = {
					who = event_target:gather_food_option_1
					value >= 50
				}
			}
			
			mult_modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				trait = temperate
			}
		}
	}
